| **v0.8** | 2026-11-18 | **Owner rounding model (14:04–14:06 PKT, `05-g1-decisions.md` §25)**, marked "⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25)"; list §1.21. (1) Tax always on the exact amounts (`pro_rata`: on the declared exact shares; `higher_rate`: as today). (2) Rounding = a separate, untaxed **rounding adjustment** owned by the restaurant, in **no payment class**; it never enters a class share, `d` or a tax base; `d*` and every rule that moved rounding into the card or cash part are removed (v0.7.3–v0.7.8 two-dues attribution, H13-1 / H14-1 / H14-2 `fix3`). (3) Every bill rounds where the jurisdiction says (Pakistan seeds `all`, Rs 1, card bills included — "har bill round ho"); `cash` and `none` stay as data for other jurisdictions, under the same rules (cash-only: the line exists only when cash is involved). (4) Close: Σ payments − change = the rounded total; `pro_rata`: \|Σ digital − `d`\| ≤ \|rounding\|; no other class check. (5) Refunds "jitna liya utna wapas": a full refund returns exactly what each type paid, rounding included; a partial refund re-rounds the new total and returns paid − new total, per type up to what that type paid; never re-taxed by the refund's type. (6) The rounding line reaches the authority only as the adapter maps it, never inside taxable value (Appendix P; new authority question A14). Consistency pass: B9, §3.3 8, §3.5 (7)–(8), §3.6, §6.9, §6.9a, §8 pad, receipts, X / Z, tests #15e, #20b, #20c (+ full-refund case). Endpoints unchanged (32 + 3). |
| **v0.8.1** | 2026-11-18 | **Run 15** (`docs/reviews/05_v0.8_targeted_check.md`, 3 major + 1 minor regression, all in `pro_rata`) and **owner ruling 14:40 PKT (`05-g1-decisions.md` §26): `pro_rata` is out of v1.0 entirely.** v1.0 supports only `higher_rate` (all-digital → the digital rate; any cash → the cash rate on the whole split). The data shape stays (`mixed_class_rule` accepts only `higher_rate` in v1.0; `pro_rata` refused by the billing settings, `422 CONFIG_REFERENCE_INVALID { field: 'mixed_class_rule', reason: 'pro_rata_not_supported' }`); `DeclareClassShares`, `SplitClassSharesDeclared`, `bill_split_shares`, `d`, the class-share caps and tolerance, `BILL_CLASS_SHARE_MISMATCH`, `BILL_CLASS_SHARE_INVALID`, the `pro_rata` pad fields and tests leave the live text (struck through where history is useful); §3.6 becomes a deferral note; §12.7 / §12.4 list "pro rata mixed-payment tax — v1.1, when an authority requires it (A6)", starting from runs 12–15. ---

## §1. Header

### 1.1 Stories covered

- **E8** (P1): payments at checkout (mixed modes, change, manual card / online amounts, R7); split bill (by items, by amount, equally into N, each split paid and printed on its own; M-1); tax invoice (each tax with its rate, the branch's tax registration number, total incl. tax; taxes only from the item's tax profile and the jurisdiction data, never chosen at the bill, G1-15 as restated in v0.7, J-2); Z report on the hub; blind count; cashier shifts and petty cash.
- **E0** "Payments at checkout" AC1, 2, 4–6 (AC7 dropped, K-7); "Refunds, cancellations, complimentary items" (a refund after close = **reopen + credit invoice**, Q8; comp removal before billing, Q7; walk-out = 04 void, Q12); "Cashier shifts, day open / close, petty cash, Z report" (AC1–AC7; AC8 day reopen **not built**, Q11); "Receipt and ticket templates" (fixed layout with owner-edited parts, K-12); "Printers, cash drawer" (drawer kick, no-sale open).
- **E3** counter receipt AC1–3; **E6** permissions AC2 (billing permissions, K-13); audit AC2 for billing actions (04 `registerAuditAction`).
- **D8, D9, D11, D21, D39, D47, D58** as narrowed by README R7, R12, R13 (amended by R37, §4.5 row R-1), R20.
- **Fiscal** (owner direction 2026-11-17, G1-16): fiscal adapter port with an adapter per authority, PRA live from day 1 on the POS floor.
- **Jurisdiction-neutral core** ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1…J-7, owner 2026-11-18 11:41 PKT, `05-g1-decisions.md` §17): the product is a SaaS sold across Pakistani provinces and other countries; every jurisdiction rule 05 uses (taxes per profile, price mode, currency exponent, cash rounding, buyer tax ID, receipt fiscal fields, catch-up window, credit support, payment-mode codes) is **data per branch**; the PRA adapter is one plug-in (Appendix P) and the only live adapter in the pilot.
- Carried rows: README :183, :191, :202, :203, :214, :226, :240, :243, :246; 02b §4.3 To 05, §12.3 Q2; 03c §4.3 To 05; 04 §4.3 To 05, G-8; OPEN-QUESTIONS 02a-N3, 02b AD-5; F §3.5, §4.6, §7 OQ15 (§4.6 lists where each lands).

### 1.2 In scope (hub)

Bills per seating (dine-in) or per order (takeaway / parking); splits (items / amount / equal); discount on the whole bill (before the first split closes) with reason and approval (⚠️ Changed in v0.2, OQ-4); sticky service charge (applied once by rule, removable with reason and approval, re-added only by hand); tax at payment from the branch's **jurisdiction data** (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1, J-2): each line's tax profile → its ordered taxes, each rate by payment class / date / integration status; mixed-class rule; price mode inclusive or exclusive), rounding once per split by the jurisdiction's cash rounding rule; payments in any owner mode with tendered amount and change; close per split → invoice (gap-free number), at the last split `OrdersBilled` + `BillClosed`; check bill (provisional print, no number, no fiscal call); request-bill print at the floor / zone bill printer with a run-time "Print to" target; reopen a closed bill (credit invoice + new invoice at the next close); remove a comp on an unbilled line (approval); buyer name + **buyer tax ID** (`{ type, value }`, types from the jurisdiction data, ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-4)) above the jurisdiction's threshold or an approved waiver; shifts per person (float, taken-by vs drawer shift, optional handover count, blind count, variance approval); petty cash (advance + settle, cash out, cash in, categories, limit approval); drawer no-sale open; carry-over of unbilled orders; day-close guards and the Z appender; X and Z reports; fiscal port (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-6): the core holds only the port contract, §3.15), adapters `none` and `PRA` (Appendix P), the durable fiscal outbox and its worker, fiscal status and the stuck-outbox alert; receipt / check-bill / credit-note / Z `Doc` builders in `@pos/domain`.

### 1.3 Out of scope

Refund without reopen (`apply-refund` stays in the catalogue, unused; Q8). Business-day reopen (Q11). Tips (Q5). Line discounts (Q6). Card-terminal or wallet integration and reconciliation (R7, P2). Khata / customer credit, delivery charge and rider cash (P2; the sticky-charge rule already covers a delivery charge, G1-14). Raast payment QR (G1-11). Logo on the receipt (K-12). Sales by item / hour / staff, live money view, D22 tiles, cloud shift and Z history (07 / 06, K-9, Q14). Adapters SRB, KPRA, BRA, FBR-ICT (milestone C, K-14 / R37) and **any other country's adapter or jurisdiction seed** (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1): the core and the data shape are ready; only the `none` and Punjab rows are seeded). Locale / translation work beyond EN + UR, and more than **one currency per tenant** (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.3 (H10-8): was "per branch"; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7, owner §17 YAGNI). Splitting a sale to stay under the buyer-ID threshold (**never built**, Q18). Vendor payments / payables (module 10). Training mode. Bill-photo upload for petty-cash settle (G1 Q15, deferred). Any change to how 02b prices a line or 04 voids one.

### 1.4 Decisions log

| ID | Decision | Source |
|---|---|---|
| B1 | **One bill per seating (dine-in) or per `bill_key` (takeaway: the root order of its additions; parking: the plate's `table_key`)** ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-4: was per `table_key`), ⚠️ Changed in v0.5.1 (H4-6): a takeaway addition keeps **the root's token**, its own `table_key` and the root's `bill_key` (H3-1) ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-21, Q-4, developer call, owner delegated): a parking `bill_key` is **per plate per business day** (`table_key || ':' || business_date`, the normalised plate, ⚠️ Changed in v0.5.1 (H4-6)), so a car that comes back on a later day starts a new bill. ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-10 + S-9, owner R2) (was "per order": a takeaway addition is a new order with the first order's `table_key`, 02b row Q-6; parking orders of one car already share it). A bill is created by the first 05 command that touches it (`BillOpened` + `ChargeApplied`); until then every read shows the bill **projected** from 02b and the rule (same numbers). Its orders: a **reopened** bill = its `bill_orders` set plus, in full-edit mode, the seating's / key's new unbilled orders; any other open bill = the seating's / key's orders with `billed_at IS NULL` and `cancelled_at IS NULL` that are not in another open bill's `bill_orders` (v0.3 H1-21). At the bill's last close they are written to `bill_orders`. **One full-edit open bill per seating / key at a time**: after a bill closes, a new order on that seating / key opens a new bill automatically, no staff action (OQ-6, S-9 (b)). | K-1, K-3, G0 story 1, R2 |
| B2 | **Splits are parts of the bill** (`items`, `amount`, `equal`). No split plan = one split (`split_no 1`). Each split holds 1..n payments, closes on its own into **one invoice** with its own number, tax, rounding, receipt; the bill closes at its last split: `OrdersBilled { order_ids, bill_id }` (02b) and, for dine-in, `BillClosed { seating_id }` (02a stream `table`) **once**, in that transaction. | K-1, K-3, M-1, G1-2 |
| B3 | **05 never writes 02b's ledger** (G0-2): line money is 02b `orderLines` (`billed_minor`) minus 04 `pendingVoids(…).refund_minor`; 05 owns only bill-level money. A line with `return_of` is never billed (03a §3.8a: its money is 0 already). | G0-2, 04 To 05, 03a To 05 |
| B4 | **Tax is never chosen at the bill** ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2, owner §17; G1-15 restated): **a line's taxes come only from its item's tax profile and the branch's jurisdiction data**, never from a staff choice. Each line carries exactly one **tax profile** (`tax_profile_id` = the item's 01a tax class, copied from `ItemAdded.tax_class_id`, row Q-1, K-4); the jurisdiction maps the profile to an **ordered list of taxes** (§3.4). A deal line is taxed under the deal's profile on its billed amount, an option child under its own; never from `allocated_price_minor`. The checkout has **no tax buttons** and nothing is pre-applied for staff to remove; a line is never taxed under two profiles. Taxes **stack only where the jurisdiction data defines more than one tax for the profile** (e.g. a federal + a provincial tax); every Pakistan profile has **one** tax, so v0.6.2's behaviour (one tax per line, never the Mutfak 16 % + 8 % stacking, M-4) is unchanged. | G1-15, K-4, README :214, §17 |
| B5 | **The rates come from the jurisdiction's rate rows at payment / close** ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1, J-2) (row A-1): key (jurisdiction, profile's `fiscal_category`, tax code, payment class `cash` / `digital`, integration status, `effective_from` ≤ issue date), each row with its notification ref; owner overrides per (tax class, tax code) win over the seeded catalog. The seeded jurisdiction **`none`** gives every profile one tax at the tax class's `rate_bp` (v0.6.2's adapter-`none` behaviour). Choosing / switching a payment mode recomputes tax and total on the same screen. Every invoice keeps the rates it used (`invoice_taxes`), so a credit copies them, never re-reads the table. | K-15, Q16, M-7 |
| B6 | **Mixed payment classes inside one split** ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26) (owner 14:40 PKT): v1.0 supports **only `higher_rate`** — all payments digital → the digital rates; any cash payment → the cash rates on the whole split. The data shape stays (`mixed_class_rule`, per jurisdiction), but it accepts only `higher_rate` in v1.0; `pro_rata` is refused by the billing settings (row A-2) and is a v1.1 item when an authority requires it (A6; §12.7, §12.4, starting from runs 12–15). ~~v0.6.2 – v0.8 `pro_rata` (declared class amounts, `DeclareClassShares`, tolerance)~~ — out of v1.0. | Q4, G1-5, A6, §26 |
| B7 | **Discount before tax, on the whole bill only** ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-4: was "on the bill or one open split"), amount or percent, owner reason buttons + note for `other`; `apply-discount` grant (approval per grant, 01d kind `discount`); recomputed live on the whole bill and spread over **all bill lines** pro rata with `allocateCappedMinor` (⚠️ Changed in v0.3 H1-4: was `allocateDealMinor`, which can drive a line negative; G0-5 named it) **before** splitting, so each split carries its lines' shares; set and changed only while **no split is closed** (`409 BILL_PARTLY_PAID` after the first close); at the first split close its amount freezes and lines that join later carry no discount ; ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-5, owner R3): after any void / comp / remove / comp removal on the open part, the open part's discount = **min(frozen amount − Σ closed splits' D, the open part's current pre-discount total)**, never negative, re-spread over the open lines with `allocateCappedMinor`; closed splits never change; a reduction is not given back to the customer ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-8, Q-1, owner 2026-11-18 08:38 PKT): **the frozen discount covers only the lines that were on the bill when the first split closed** (`bill_discount_lines`, §5); the cap of R3 and its re-spread use only those of them still in the open part (cap = their current pre-discount total); a line that joins later is always full price, never carries a share; close refused while a discount request on the bill is open (`409 DISCOUNT_PENDING`). | Q6, OQ-4, G0-5, G0-6, M-9 |
| B8 | **Charges are sticky.** The service charge rate of the order type is written once at bill creation (`ChargeApplied { source: 'rule' }`); `RemoveServiceCharge` (approvable, reason) writes `ChargeRemoved`; no read, print, split, merge, move or reopen recomputes it from the rule; re-adding is an explicit `ApplyServiceCharge` (audited). | Q5, G1-14, M-3 |
| B9 | **Rounding adjustment** ⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25) (owner: "har bill round ho"): the total is rounded once per split by the jurisdiction's rule `{ step_minor, mode: 'nearest' \| 'down' \| 'up', applies_to: 'all' \| 'cash' }` (⚠️ v0.7 J-1; per-jurisdiction data, owner-editable per branch, ⚠️ v0.7.2 H9-3; a `NULL` step = 1 minor unit = no rounding, the `none` seed). The rounding is a **separate, untaxed line owned by the restaurant, in no payment class**: tax is always on the exact amounts; the rounding never enters a class share, `d` or a tax base. Pakistan seeds (`PK`, `PK-PB`): Rs 1 = 100 paisa, `nearest`, half up, `applies_to: 'all'` — **every bill rounds, card bills included** (v0.6.2's values). `applies_to: 'cash'` (other jurisdictions, data only): the line exists only when the split has a cash payment. ~~v0.7.2–v0.7.8 card / cash rounding attribution (H9-2, H10-1, H10-2)~~ — superseded by §25. Printed as its own line ("Rounding adjustment"). All arithmetic in integers (BigInt where products can pass 2^53), half up at each named point only (§3.5). | D47, M-5, §17, §25 |
| B10 | **Payments** `{ mode_id, amount_minor, tendered_minor?, has_reference, taken_by_staff_id, shift_id }` ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-2, H1-20); any mode always available (K-7), reference required per mode (data); `amount_minor` is the money applied and never exceeds the split's balance (cash too); a cash `tendered_minor ≥ amount_minor` is what was handed over and its excess is the change, fixed at `AddPayment`; the reference (1–60 free text) lives only in the hub table `payment_references`, never in an event; removable before close (`take-payment`, reason, audited). | G1-2, K-7, M-6, H1-2, H1-20 |
| B11 | **Taken-by vs shift.** `taken_by_staff_id` = the signed-in person; `shift_id` = the shift whose money it is: on a device that is a **drawer** (has an open drawer shift) every payment lands in that drawer's shift, whoever takes it; on any other device the taker's own open shift. Cash on a device with no drawer shift and no own cash shift → `409 SHIFT_NOT_OPEN`. ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-3), ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT): a money shift is a shift in state **`open`**; counting closes the shift at once (§3.11), so no counted shift can take money, and the drawer is free for the next shift immediately. | Q9, Q10, G1-6, README :183, H6-3 |
| B12 | **Close is online-only** (never device-queued), checked in one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction: pending void (04), pending weight (`live_units > 0`), open discount request, balance ≠ 0, buyer ID, screen total stale (`expected_total_minor`). Printer and internet are never on the close path. | G0-1, D11, 01c V18, R8, E5 AC1 |
| B13 | **Invoice numbers** are hub-assigned, per branch, gap-free, continuous across days, two series: `sale` and `credit` (G1-3). Assigned inside the close / reopen transaction as `MAX + 1` of the series (single writer, D45). | G0-3, G1-3 |
| B14 | (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-6, Q-3 (b), owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT): a reopened bill with no live line left — every order cancelled — and every split's balance 0 closes with **no new invoice**: `BillClosedEmpty` (payload §6.29), state CLOSED, no `OrdersBilled`, no fiscal row; the credit alone stands for the authority.) **Reopen = full edit for every order type** (⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-19): a reopen clears the discount's frozen amount; the discount is the bill's again until the next first close; ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-5): takeaway / parking full edit only while no other open bill holds the key, §3.10) ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-10 + S-9, owner R2) (`ReopenBill`, `reopen-bill`, approvable, reason): one `InvoiceCredited` per sale invoice of the bill (credit series, fiscal outbox ordered behind its original), 02b `OrdersUnbilled` (row Q-2, OQ-1), every split open again with its payments; lines are then added, voided, comped and removed through 02b / 04 as usual, discount and payments through 05; the next close of each split issues a **new** invoice number. **Dine-in:** full edit needs the seating re-opened (02a `SeatingReopened`, row T-3) — possible while the table has not been cleaned and no other open bill is on the seating; otherwise the reopen is **corrections only** (no new lines: 02a's `TABLE_SEATING_CHANGED`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 H2-14) (§3.10). A business day is never reopened; a bill from an earlier day may be reopened and its credit and new invoice count in **today's** Z (an earlier-day **takeaway / parking** reopen is corrections only, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (Q-3, developer call, owner delegated)). At a re-close, `BillClosed` is written when the seating is open and holds no other open bill (so a fully reopened table goes back to NEEDS_CLEANING); none otherwise. | Q8, Q11, G1-3, R2 |
| B15 | **A closed split is frozen; the rest stays open** ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6: was "the whole partly paid bill is frozen"). The units a closed split invoiced (item split: its assigned units; amount / equal split: every line's allocated share, so every line that existed at that close) can no longer change: 02b `RemoveItems` / `CompLine` / `SetLineWeight`, 05 `RemoveComp` and 04 `VoidItems` touching them → `409 LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT` (line guard, rows Q-3, V-1); a change to a closed split is a reopen (Q8). Lines not in a closed split stay editable. **A new line ordered after a split closed joins the open remainder automatically** (the highest-numbered open split; no staff action). All splits closed = the bill is CLOSED; a later item on that seating opens a new bill (B1). Invariants: Σ closed-split units + open-remainder units = the bill's live line units; no unit in two splits. | OQ-6, G0-4 |
| B16 | **Takeaway / parking close any time after the first Send** (Q2): before the food, after it, or when a phone customer arrives. ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-10 + S-9, owner R2): **before close** — forgot an item / customer wants more → `SubmitOrder` with `adds_to_order_id` (row Q-6: **the root order's token**, its own `table_key`, the root's `bill_key`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-1, owner 09:26 PKT)) joins the open bill; wrong item → 02b `RemoveItems` (not started) or 04 `VoidItems` (started, approval per grant) and add the right one; too many units → `RemoveItems` / `VoidItems` with `units`. **After close** → `ReopenBill` (approval per grant) → the same actions on the reopened bill → close again (credit + new invoice number). No lock for these types (02b §3.1), so nothing in 02a is involved. | Q2, G1-4, R2 |
| B17 | **Check bill** = a print, not a money event: built from the live bill by a pure `Doc` builder; banner `PROVISIONAL - NOT A TAX INVOICE`; never an invoice number, fiscal number or QR; total at the **default payment class** (cash) stated on the slip (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-4): the rounded cash total, `cash_due` of the unpaid split); one `CheckBillPrinted` event (audit, ids and counts). `RequestBill` (02a) prints it at the bill printer of the table's zone / floor; no 01d notice, no push to the cashier; the cashier's open-bills list shows "check bill printed" as a pulled status. | G1-1, G1-10, Q2, Q13 |
| B18 | **Print to.** A bill document whose job fails or waits (03c status) can be re-targeted at run time to another printer (`RetargetBillPrint`, 03c row P-2): the pending job moves; nothing prints twice. | G1-10, Q13 |
| B19 | **Buyer tax ID on the whole bill.** ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-4): the rule is jurisdiction data `buyer_id { threshold_minor \| null, accepted_types: [{ type, label, pattern, strip }] }` (`null` threshold = never asked; Pakistan seed: Rs 50,000 = 5 000 000 paisa, types `CNIC` (13 digits, dashes stripped) and `NTN` (7–9 alphanumerics) — v0.6.2's rule); the buyer's ID is **`buyer_tax_id { type, value }`** with `type` ∈ the branch's accepted types, and **every privacy rule below applies to every type**. Threshold on the pre-tax net after discount (inclusive prices: the net at the default class, §3.5), checked on the **whole bill's** net (every split, closed or not, incl. after a reopen), never per split. Above it each split's close needs `{ name, tax_id: { type, value } }` or an approved waiver (`WaiveBuyerId`, `close-without-buyer-id`, approvable) → invoice `buyer_id_refused = true`. The ID value lives **only** in the hub table `invoice_buyers` read by the adapter and the receipt builder (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-5, Q-1 (a), owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT): the **full** ID number prints **only on that customer's receipt** — the original and reprints of that invoice — when the authority requires it above the threshold; never on a screen, a check bill, X / Z, a log, an event, an alert, an audit row or a hash; reprints keep today's permission; ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:21 PKT, Q-2 / Q-3): the stored print copy (`print_jobs.payload`) keeps only the **last 4** — the worker fills the full number at send from `invoice_buyers` (03c row P-5) and never stores it; a reprint rebuilds it from `invoice_buyers`, never from the print queue; the **credit note prints the last 4 only**, the full number goes to the authority inside the credit submission): never in an event, a log line, an alert, an audit row, the Z or a response. **No feature splits a sale to stay under the threshold.** ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-16, owner R5): when the whole bill crosses the threshold only after a split closed without buyer data (a later round, OQ-6), the buyer ID (or the approved waiver) is required **from the next split close on**; closed splits and their invoices are untouched. | Q18, G1-9, A7, R5 |
| B20 | **Fiscal port** ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-6): the core knows only the port contract (§3.15): `FiscalAdapter { key, capabilities: { ordering, credit }, submitSale(sub), submitCredit(sub), health() }`, one neutral submission shape, one answer shape, the idempotency key = `invoice_ref`. Registered at startup by key (`none`, and `PRA` in the pilot, Appendix P); the branch's **jurisdiction** names its adapter key; the hub posts directly over HTTPS from the branch internet (K-16). Every sale / credit invoice of a fiscalized order type gets one `fiscal_outbox` row in its transaction; the worker sends at least once with the idempotency key, in order when the adapter's `ordering` capability says so; the answer is an event (`FiscalNumberAssigned`), so receipts and reprints read it from the log. | K-14, K-16, K-17, G0-10, G1-12, G1-16 |
| B21 | **Offline / catch-up.** Close never waits for the authority. A receipt printed before the answer shows the local invoice number + **"FISCAL NUMBER PENDING"** + date / time (Appendix P A2 default); a reprint after the answer shows the fiscal number (with the jurisdiction's label) and the QR when the jurisdiction's receipt fields ask for one. Catch-up window per jurisdiction as data (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1); Pakistan seed 7 days); a row older than `stuck_after_ms` (data, default 30 min) or past half the window raises the 01d `fiscal_outbox_stuck` alert to managers (reporting the fault to the authority is theirs). | K-17, A2 |
| B22 | **Fiscal device ID (`pos_id`)** — the authority's register / POS identifier — per hub by default, optional per-device mapping (device → `pos_id`) in the branch fiscal settings; the credential (token) per `pos_id` is a **hub secret entered on the hub admin page** ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-3 (b): was "hub config file by CLI"): `SetFiscalToken` (admin session, permission `manage-fiscal-settings`, Owner only by default, row B-1); **write-only** — no query or response ever returns it, the page shows only "set on <date>"; replacing = entering a new one; stored only in the hub table `fiscal_credentials` (never synced, never logged, never in an event; the audit event `FiscalTokenSet` carries ids and the time only); never in the repo, the spec or the cloud. | K-18, A1, G1-12, OQ-3 |
| B23 | **Shift per person.** `OpenShift` (float, 0 allowed); a drawer device holds at most one open shift; blind count server-enforced (no response carries `expected_minor` before the shift's `ShiftCounted`). ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT): **`CountShift` closes the shift at once and frees the drawer**; a variance above the branch threshold is recorded on the closed shift and raises a 01d `shift_variance` request that only **signs it off** (approve → `approved`, reject → `disputed`, shown on X / Z; no recount, no reopen); it never holds the drawer and never blocks the day close (Z: "pending sign-off"). `skip-blind-count` (Owner by default) closes without a count, always audited; optional `HandoverCount` on a drawer shift (variance recorded, shift stays open). | D21, Q9, Q10, G1-6, Q-2 |
| B24 | **Petty cash is cash only, on a shift:** advance to a named staff member (open until settled: spent + change returned), direct cash-out (category, paid to, note), cash in; categories owner-edited; above the branch limit, or with a `requires_approval` grant → 01d `petty_cash` request. | Q15, G1-8 |
| B25 | **Day close stays 01a's `CloseBusinessDay`** (rows A-3, A-4): 05 registers guards (open shifts; unbilled, uncancelled, not carried-over orders) and an appender that closes 05's open requests `obsolete` and writes `ZReportIssued` before `BusinessDayClosed`, in the same transaction; the command moves to `close-business-day` and is also offered on the staff app (row H-1). `CarryOverOrders` (manager, reason) lets listed orders survive the close; they bill next day and show on that day's Z. Void requests still block (04). | K-5, Q11, M-8 |
| B26 | **Reports on the hub:** X (the open day, any time) and Z (one closed day) from the same pure builder over 05's projections; Z = Σ sale invoices − Σ credit invoices issued in the day, by payment mode, tax by class, discounts / comps / voids / reopens with requester, approver, method; per shift float / expected / counted / variance; open advances. Sales by item / hour / staff → 07. | Q14, K-9, G0 story 9 |
| B27 | **Remove comp** on an **unbilled** line (`RemoveComp`, `remove-comp`, approvable, reason) → 02b's new `LineCompRemoved` (row Q-4; 02b owns the event and its fold). A billed line's comp changes only through a reopen. | Q7, G1-7 |
| B28 | **Walk-out** = void the unpaid lines through 04 (reason `walk_out`, row V-2), no "unpaid" payment mode. | Q12 |
| B29 | **Receipt template** = a fixed layout with owner-edited header / footer lines, show / hide toggles and variables (`{branch_name}`, `{tax_reg_no}` (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-7): was `{ntn}`; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-5): prints **every** seller registration of the invoice's snapshot (⚠️ v0.7.3 H10-8: the branch's at issue, §6.12 `fiscal.seller_registrations`; a reprint uses the snapshot), one line each, `<label>: <number>` with the jurisdiction's label per code; Pakistan one line "NTN: …"), `{address}`, `{phone}`, `{bill_no}`, `{date}`, `{cashier}`); the fiscal block's fields come from the jurisdiction's `receipt_fiscal` data (J-1); no logo; preview in the owner app with `renderText` moved to `@pos/domain` (row P-3). | K-12, R12 |
| B30 | **Logs carry ids and counts only** — never names, PINs, phone numbers, buyer tax IDs of any type, tokens, amounts tied to a named person. ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-22): **no free text in an event** — notes, `paid_to` and references live in hub-only tables (`bill_texts`, `payment_references`), never synced; events carry `has_note` / `has_paid_to` / `has_reference`. | owner rule, G1-9, H2-22 |
| B31 | **Jurisdiction per branch** ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1, owner §17): each branch's billing settings name one `jurisdiction_id` from a seeded, versioned cloud catalog (row A-1); the jurisdiction's data decide the adapter key, the currency (whose ISO 4217 minor-unit **exponent** sets "one major unit" = `10^exponent` minor units; never a hard-coded 2), the default price mode (inclusive / exclusive), the cash rounding rule, the mixed-class rule, the tax list per profile and its rate rows, the buyer tax ID rule, the receipt fiscal fields, the catch-up window and the credit support. The owner may override, per branch, exactly what v0.6.2 let him set: price mode, rounding step and mode, rate rows, integration status (A-1). **Seeded** (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-3)): `none` (no authority, adapter `none`, the tenant's currency, no rounding, one tax per profile at `rate_bp`), **`PK`** (Pakistan, no authority: adapter `none`, PKR, Rs 1 rounding, no buyer threshold, one tax per profile at `rate_bp` — v0.6.2's adapter-`none` behaviour exactly) and `PK-PB` (Punjab, adapter `PRA`, PKR — v0.6.2's PRA behaviour exactly); the pilot's "start on `none`, switch to PRA" is a switch **`PK` → `PK-PB`**, recorded with `switched_at`. Hub default with no `billing` key: the built-in rules equal to `PK` for a PKR **tenant**, to `none` otherwise (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.3 (H10-4), A-1 (5)). Every invoice records its `jurisdiction_id`, adapter key and currency **and a snapshot of the jurisdiction data it was issued under** (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-6): credit support, adapter capabilities, receipt labels, QR template, seller registrations, payment codes), so a credit, a credit note and a reprint always follow the original (H6-8), never today's data. ~~⚠️ Changed in v0.7.1 (OQ-9): currency per branch~~ — ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-1, owner Q-1 2026-11-18 12:18 PKT, supersedes OQ-9): **the currency is per tenant** (`tenants.currency_code`, F §6, row F-6): one tenant = one country / currency; every branch's jurisdiction must use the tenant's currency (checked when the billing settings choose a jurisdiction, row A-1); an owner with branches in two countries opens a second tenant. Everything 05 computes, stores and reports is one branch's, so always one currency; invoices still record `currency_code`. | §17, §18, K-15, Q16 |
| B32 | **Words** ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-7): core text says authority, jurisdiction, buyer tax ID, fiscal number, fiscal device ID; "PRA", "CNIC", "NTN", "USIN", "POS ID" as PRA terms appear only in Appendix P, in the Pakistan seed data and in history rows. | §17 |

### 1.21 Owner rounding model (v0.8, §25) ⚠️ Changed in v0.8

| # | Owner rule (§25) | Where |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Tax is computed on the exact (unrounded) amounts; under `pro_rata` on the declared exact class shares (`d` digital, the rest cash); under `higher_rate` as before. | §3.5 (3)–(6), (8); §3.6 rule 2 |
| 2 | Rounding is a separate, untaxed **rounding adjustment** line owned by the restaurant, in **no payment class**; it never enters a class share, `d` or any tax base. `d*` and all text that moved rounding into the card or cash part are removed. | B9, §3.5 (7), §3.6 rules 1, 3–5 |
| 3 | Every bill rounds where the jurisdiction says so (Pakistan seeds `applies_to: 'all'`, Rs 1, card bills included). `cash` and `none` stay as data for other jurisdictions under rules 1–2 (cash-only: the line exists only when cash is involved). | B9, §3.5 (7) |
| 4 | Close: Σ payments − change = the rounded total; under `pro_rata` \|Σ digital − `d`\| ≤ \|rounding line\|; the rounding is settled by whichever payments cover the total; no other class check. | §3.3 8, §3.6 rule 4 |
| 5 | Refunds "jitna liya utna wapas": a full refund returns exactly what was taken, per type, rounding included; a partial refund re-rounds the bill and returns paid − new rounded total, per type up to what that type paid; the tax of what was paid is never re-computed by the refund's type. | §3.5 (7), §6.9 |
| 6 | The rounding line goes to the authority only as the adapter maps it, never inside taxable value. | §3.15, Appendix P §P.2, A14 |
| — | Removed (existed only for the old attribution) | v0.7.3–v0.7.8: card / cash dues with rounding on the cash part, `PAYMENT_CASH_STEP` kept only for cash-only rounding, `d*` (H13-1), "stays all-digital" (H14-1), the `pro_rata` cash refund cap (H14-2), the `'all'` rule-1 refusal (H12-1), card `in` / `out` beyond the balance by `R` (H11-1, H12-6). |
