## §5. Data model (hub SQLite) — `apps/hub/src/db/migrations/0023_billing.sql`

Two projections, both **events-only** (they read only their own tables and the event; never 02b's or 02a's tables in a fold), with `reset`, registered after `orders`, `tables` and `audit`:

- **`billing`** — ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-15): also 02b `ItemVoided`, `ItemRemoved` (a leaving unit off the highest open split, §3.8) and `SeatingSplit` by `order_id`; `BillOpened`, `ChargeApplied`, `ChargeRemoved`, `CheckBillPrinted`, `BillSplitsSet`, `DiscountApplied`, `DiscountRemoved`, `PaymentAdded`, `PaymentRemoved`, `BuyerIdWaived`, `InvoiceIssued` (+ `closed_split_units`), ~~`SplitClassSharesDeclared`~~ (v0.8.1 §26), `BillReopened`, `InvoiceCredited`, `FiscalNumberAssigned`, `FiscalSubmitRejected`, `FiscalTokenSet`, `OrdersCarriedOver`, `ZReportIssued`, `BillClosedEmpty` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-10)), 02a `TablesMerged` / `SeatingSplit` (bill re-pointing, §3.9), `BusinessDayOpened` (current day key).
- **`shifts`** — `ShiftOpened`, `ShiftCounted`, `ShiftCountSkipped`, `HandoverCounted`, `ShiftClosed`, `PettyCashAdvanced`, `PettyCashSettled`, `PettyCashOut`, `PettyCashIn`, `DrawerOpened`, and the money facts of `PaymentAdded` / `PaymentRemoved` / `InvoiceIssued` (cash tendered, change) for the running expected cash. ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-1, H8-10): also `ShiftVarianceSignedOff` (sets `variance_state`, inserts a `shift_signoffs` row).

Hub state that is **not** a projection (plain statements, never events, never synced; 03c rule): `invoice_buyers` (command-written), `payment_references` (command-written, v0.3 H1-20), `fiscal_credentials` (command-written, v0.2 OQ-3), `bill_print_jobs` (written with each print), and the worker columns of `fiscal_outbox`.

```sql
-- projection billing ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE bills (
  bill_id            TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  scope              TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (scope IN ('seating','key')),   -- v0.3 R2: 'key' = takeaway / parking table_key
  seating_id         TEXT,                                  -- dine_in
  table_key          TEXT,                                  -- takeaway / parking: 02b orders.bill_key (v0.4 H2-4, H2-16; column name kept)
  order_type         TEXT NOT NULL,
  state              TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (state IN ('open','partly_paid','closed')),
  opened_at          INTEGER NOT NULL,
  closed_at          INTEGER,
  sc_bp              INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,            -- ChargeApplied (rule or manual); 0 after ChargeRemoved
  sc_removed         INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (sc_removed IN (0,1)),   -- sticky (B8)
  discount_json      TEXT,                                  -- { kind, value, reason_code, alert_id, frozen_minor|null } | NULL; whole bill only (v0.2, OQ-4)
  split_mode         TEXT CHECK (split_mode IN ('items','amount','equal')),    -- NULL = one split
  buyer_waived       INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (buyer_waived IN (0,1)),
  check_printed_at   INTEGER,                               -- pulled status "check bill printed" (B17)
  reopen_count       INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  reopen_mode        TEXT CHECK (reopen_mode IN ('full','corrections')),   -- v0.3 R2 (§3.10); NULL = never reopened
  absorbed_into      TEXT,                                  -- §3.9 merge
  prices_include_tax INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (prices_include_tax IN (0,1)),   -- v0.7.2 (H9-9): the mode in force at BillOpened, frozen for the bill
  rounding_json      TEXT NOT NULL,                         -- v0.8.2 (H16-2): { step_minor|null, mode, applies_to } frozen at BillOpened
  CHECK ((scope = 'seating') = (seating_id IS NOT NULL)),
  CHECK ((scope = 'key') = (table_key IS NOT NULL))
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX bills_live_seating ON bills (seating_id) WHERE seating_id IS NOT NULL AND absorbed_into IS NULL AND state <> 'closed' AND reopen_mode IS NOT 'corrections';  -- openBillOf(seating); one open bill per seating, a later one after close (v0.2, OQ-6)
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX bills_live_key     ON bills (table_key) WHERE table_key IS NOT NULL AND state <> 'closed' AND reopen_mode IS NOT 'corrections';  -- openBillOf(key) (v0.3 R2)
CREATE INDEX bills_open                ON bills (state, opened_at) WHERE state <> 'closed' AND absorbed_into IS NULL;  -- open-bills list, X flag

CREATE TABLE bill_orders (              -- written at the bill's last close (B1); the reopen set (v0.2, OQ-6)
  bill_id TEXT NOT NULL, order_id TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY (bill_id, order_id)
);
CREATE INDEX bill_orders_order ON bill_orders (order_id);                                  -- line guard (row Q-3)

CREATE TABLE bill_splits (
  bill_id   TEXT NOT NULL, split_no INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (split_no BETWEEN 1 AND 20),
  plan_json TEXT,                                          -- amount: { share_minor }; equal: { n, k }; items: rows in bill_split_lines (v0.4, H2-15)
  state     TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (state IN ('open','closed')),
  invoice_id TEXT,                                         -- the current sale invoice when closed
  PRIMARY KEY (bill_id, split_no)
);

CREATE TABLE bill_discount_lines (      -- v0.4 (H2-8, owner Q-1): lines on the bill at the first split close; only they carry the frozen discount
  bill_id TEXT NOT NULL, order_item_id TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY (bill_id, order_item_id)
);                                        -- filled from the first InvoiceIssued's discount_line_ids (one INSERT … SELECT json_each); cleared by BillReopened (H2-19)

-- bill_split_shares removed in v0.8.1 (§26: pro_rata out of v1.0)

CREATE TABLE bill_split_lines (         -- v0.4 (H2-15): item-split plan entries, foldable from events alone
  bill_id TEXT NOT NULL, split_no INTEGER NOT NULL, order_item_id TEXT NOT NULL, order_id TEXT NOT NULL, units INTEGER NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (bill_id, split_no, order_item_id)
);
CREATE INDEX bill_split_lines_line  ON bill_split_lines (order_item_id);              -- ItemVoided / ItemRemoved fold
CREATE INDEX bill_split_lines_order ON bill_split_lines (order_id);                   -- SeatingSplit fold

CREATE TABLE bill_payments (
  payment_id        TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  bill_id           TEXT NOT NULL, split_no INTEGER NOT NULL,
  mode_id           TEXT NOT NULL,
  payment_class     TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (payment_class IN ('cash','digital')),
  direction         TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (direction IN ('in','out')),
  amount_minor      INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (amount_minor > 0),
  tendered_minor    INTEGER CHECK (tendered_minor IS NULL OR tendered_minor >= amount_minor),   -- change = tendered − amount, on this row's shift (v0.3, H1-2, H1-3)
  has_reference     INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (has_reference IN (0,1)),   -- v0.3 (H1-20): the text is in payment_references
  taken_by_staff_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  shift_id          TEXT NOT NULL,
  added_at          INTEGER NOT NULL,
  removed_at        INTEGER,
  invoice_id        TEXT                                   -- set by InvoiceIssued; cleared by BillReopened (carried to the next close)
);
CREATE INDEX bill_payments_split ON bill_payments (bill_id, split_no) WHERE removed_at IS NULL;   -- bill view, close

CREATE TABLE invoices (
  invoice_id      TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  series          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (series IN ('sale','credit')),
  number          INTEGER NOT NULL,                        -- gap-free per series (B13)
  invoice_ref     TEXT NOT NULL,                           -- '<prefix>' + 'S' | 'C' + number = the fiscal idempotency key (v0.6, H6-1; v0.7 J-6)
  bill_id         TEXT NOT NULL, split_no INTEGER NOT NULL,
  ref_invoice_id  TEXT,                                    -- credit → original
  business_date   TEXT NOT NULL,                           -- the open day at issue (Z key)
  issued_at       INTEGER NOT NULL,
  order_type      TEXT NOT NULL,
  jurisdiction_id TEXT NOT NULL, adapter_key TEXT NOT NULL,   -- v0.7 (J-1): recorded at issue; a credit copies its original's
  currency_code   TEXT NOT NULL,                           -- v0.7 (J-1): the tenant's currency at issue (v0.7.2 owner Q-1: one tenant = one currency)
  prices_include_tax INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (prices_include_tax IN (0,1)),   -- v0.7 (J-3): the price mode used
  subtotal_minor  INTEGER NOT NULL, discount_minor INTEGER NOT NULL, service_charge_minor INTEGER NOT NULL,
  tax_minor       INTEGER NOT NULL, rounding_minor INTEGER NOT NULL, total_minor INTEGER NOT NULL,
  change_minor    INTEGER NOT NULL,
  payment_class   TEXT NOT NULL,                           -- the split's class, or 'mixed'
  has_buyer_id    INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (has_buyer_id IN (0,1)),
  buyer_id_refused INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (buyer_id_refused IN (0,1)),
  closed_by       TEXT NOT NULL,
  doc_json        TEXT NOT NULL,                           -- lines + tax rows + payments as in the event (receipt reprint, Z)
  credited_by     TEXT,                                    -- the credit invoice_id once credited
  fiscal_number   TEXT, qr_payload TEXT                    -- FiscalNumberAssigned
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX invoices_series_number ON invoices (series, number);                  -- MAX+1, gap check
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX invoices_ref ON invoices (invoice_ref);                             -- v0.6 (H6-1): idempotency key unique across sale and credit
CREATE INDEX invoices_day   ON invoices (business_date, series);                         -- Z / X
CREATE INDEX invoices_bill  ON invoices (bill_id);                                       -- reopen, bill view

CREATE TABLE invoice_taxes (            -- v0.7 (J-2): one row per (profile, tax, payment class) — the rate snapshot of the invoice
  invoice_id TEXT NOT NULL, tax_profile_id TEXT NOT NULL, tax_code TEXT NOT NULL, payment_class TEXT NOT NULL,
  seq INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (seq BETWEEN 1 AND 4), compound INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (compound IN (0,1)), label TEXT NOT NULL,
  rate_bp INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (rate_bp BETWEEN 0 AND 10000), base_minor INTEGER NOT NULL, tax_minor INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (tax_minor >= 0), rate_ref TEXT,
  PRIMARY KEY (invoice_id, tax_profile_id, tax_code, payment_class)
);                                                                                       -- Z tax by (tax code, profile, class) (joined by invoices_day)

CREATE TABLE fiscal_outbox (            -- rows by the billing fold (live + rebuild, INSERT OR IGNORE); worker columns below
  invoice_id   TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  ord          INTEGER NOT NULL,                           -- the invoice event's seq (send order)
  ref_invoice_id TEXT,                                     -- credit waits for its original
  jurisdiction_id TEXT NOT NULL, adapter_key TEXT NOT NULL, pos_id TEXT,   -- v0.7 (J-1, J-6): was `authority`
  state        TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (state IN ('pending','acked','rejected','not_required')),
  queued_at    INTEGER NOT NULL,
  acked_at     INTEGER,
  -- worker-owned (plain statements; reset by a rebuild, which is harmless)
  attempts     INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, next_try_at INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  last_error   TEXT,                                       -- a short code only ('timeout', 'http_503', 'token_missing', …)
  in_flight_at INTEGER
);
CREATE INDEX fiscal_outbox_pending ON fiscal_outbox (ord) WHERE state = 'pending';       -- worker head, status, stuck check

CREATE TABLE closed_split_units (       -- v0.2 (OQ-6): units each closed split invoiced; deleted for the bill by BillReopened.
                                         -- v0.3 (H1-27): filled by one INSERT … SELECT … FROM json_each(InvoiceIssued.lines);
                                         -- amount / equal splits store the line's live units at that close (the whole line freezes)
  bill_id TEXT NOT NULL, split_no INTEGER NOT NULL, order_item_id TEXT NOT NULL, units INTEGER NOT NULL, share_minor INTEGER NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (bill_id, split_no, order_item_id)
);
CREATE INDEX closed_split_units_line ON closed_split_units (order_item_id);             -- 05 line guard (row Q-3), 1 statement

CREATE TABLE carried_orders (order_id TEXT NOT NULL, business_date TEXT NOT NULL, reason_code TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY (order_id, business_date));  -- v0.6 (H6-10)  -- day-close guard
CREATE TABLE z_reports (business_date TEXT PRIMARY KEY, z_no INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE, issued_at INTEGER NOT NULL, report_json TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE billing_day (k INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (k = 1), business_date TEXT, opened_at INTEGER);  -- current day key

-- projection shifts ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE shifts (
  shift_id        TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  staff_id        TEXT NOT NULL,
  drawer_device_id TEXT,                                   -- non-NULL = drawer shift (B11)
  business_date   TEXT NOT NULL,
  opened_at       INTEGER NOT NULL,
  float_minor     INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (float_minor >= 0),
  state           TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (state IN ('open','closed')),   -- v0.6 (owner Q-2): no counted state
  variance_state  TEXT CHECK (variance_state IN ('pending','approved','disputed')),   -- NULL = within threshold or skipped
  expected_cash_minor INTEGER NOT NULL,                    -- running (§3.7); never returned before counted_at (B23)
  counted_minor   INTEGER, counted_at INTEGER, variance_minor INTEGER,
  count_skipped   INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (count_skipped IN (0,1)),
  variance_alert_id TEXT,
  closed_at       INTEGER, closed_by TEXT
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX shifts_open_staff  ON shifts (staff_id) WHERE state = 'open';                       -- SHIFT_ALREADY_OPEN
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX shifts_open_drawer ON shifts (drawer_device_id) WHERE state = 'open' AND drawer_device_id IS NOT NULL;  -- DRAWER_IN_USE
CREATE INDEX shifts_day ON shifts (business_date);
CREATE INDEX shifts_signoff_pending ON shifts (closed_at) WHERE variance_state = 'pending';   -- v0.6.2 (H8-1): "pending sign-offs" on X / Z
CREATE TABLE shift_signoffs (            -- v0.6.2 (H8-1): "variance sign-offs" of the report's day, any shift date
  shift_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, outcome TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (outcome IN ('approved','disputed')),
  approver_staff_id TEXT NOT NULL, signed_at INTEGER NOT NULL, business_date TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX shift_signoffs_day ON shift_signoffs (business_date);                                                         -- Z / X, day-close guard

CREATE TABLE shift_handovers (shift_id TEXT NOT NULL, at INTEGER NOT NULL, counted_minor INTEGER NOT NULL,
  expected_minor INTEGER NOT NULL, variance_minor INTEGER NOT NULL, by_staff_id TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY (shift_id, at));

CREATE TABLE shift_takings (            -- per shift × mode × taker (X / Z / shift close breakdown, Q10)
  shift_id TEXT NOT NULL, mode_id TEXT NOT NULL, taken_by_staff_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  in_minor INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, out_minor INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, change_minor INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  PRIMARY KEY (shift_id, mode_id, taken_by_staff_id)
);

CREATE TABLE petty_cash (
  entry_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, shift_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('advance','out','in')),
  amount_minor INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (amount_minor > 0),
  category_id TEXT, advance_staff_id TEXT, at INTEGER NOT NULL,   -- v0.5.1 (H4-4): paid_to / note columns dropped (texts in bill_texts)
  settled_at INTEGER, settled_shift_id TEXT, spent_minor INTEGER, returned_minor INTEGER
);
CREATE INDEX petty_cash_shift ON petty_cash (shift_id);                                  -- shift view, Z
CREATE INDEX petty_cash_open  ON petty_cash (at) WHERE kind = 'advance' AND settled_at IS NULL;   -- open advances list

-- hub state (not projections) ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE TABLE invoice_buyers (           -- B19: the only place a buyer tax ID lives; written by CloseSplit; never synced, logged or returned
  invoice_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, buyer_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  id_type TEXT NOT NULL,                   -- v0.7 (J-4): a type of the jurisdiction's accepted_types ('CNIC', 'NTN', …)
  id_value TEXT NOT NULL                   -- normalised (strip characters removed); never leaves the hub except to the adapter and that receipt
);
CREATE TABLE bill_texts (               -- v0.4 (H2-22), v0.5 (H3-5): every free text a 05 command takes; hub-only, never synced
  subject_id TEXT NOT NULL,              -- the id the command mints and its event carries (entry_id, payment_id, note_id)
  field TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (field IN ('note','paid_to')), text TEXT NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (subject_id, field)
);
CREATE TABLE payment_references (       -- v0.3 (H1-20): hub-only; written by AddPayment; never synced, logged or in an event
  payment_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, reference TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE fiscal_credentials (       -- v0.2 (OQ-3, B22): written only by SetFiscalToken; never synced, logged, returned or put in an event
  pos_id TEXT NOT NULL, name TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'token',   -- v0.7.3 (H10-5): an adapter's named secrets (credentialNames); PRA: 'token'
  token TEXT NOT NULL, set_at INTEGER NOT NULL, set_by_staff_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (pos_id, name)
);
CREATE TABLE bill_print_jobs (          -- B18: job ids per bill document (03c jobs are not events)
  job_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, bill_id TEXT, doc_kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (doc_kind IN ('check','receipt','credit','report','no_sale')),
  ref_id TEXT, created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX bill_print_jobs_bill ON bill_print_jobs (bill_id, created_at DESC);        -- bill view print status
```

**Why these shapes (YAGNI):** `bills.sc_removed` + `ChargeApplied` / `ChargeRemoved` is the M-3 fix (a re-applied charge after removal is the observed failure); `invoices_series_number` makes a duplicate number impossible even under a bug; `fiscal_outbox` is the F §3.5 queue — without it a lost or doubled invoice is a legal fault (PRA s.48) and a close would have to wait for the internet (E5 AC1); `invoice_buyers` exists only because the buyer tax ID must reach the authority and the receipt but must not enter the immutable, synced event log (G1-9); `invoice_taxes` keyed by (profile, tax code, class) and the invoice's `jurisdiction_id` / `currency_code` / `prices_include_tax` exist because a money table cannot gain them after merge and a credit must copy exactly what the original used (v0.7, §1.13); `shift_takings` keeps the shift-close and Z breakdown by taker at one statement (Q10) instead of a scan of payments; `billing_day` lets the fold key invoices and shifts to the open day without reading the engine state. No soft delete, no version columns: every change is an event.

**Audit refold** ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-8): `0023_billing.sql` ends with `DELETE FROM projection_checkpoints WHERE projection = 'audit'` so `audit` refolds from seq 0 with 05's maps (00 v1.10 H-2 safe).

**Retention:** none of these rows is pruned in the pilot (money and fiscal records); `invoice_buyers` is kept as long as its invoice, hub-only, never synced ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-8). `fiscal_credentials` is in the hub database, so it is in the encrypted USB backup (00 `encryptFile`) and nowhere else. `bill_print_jobs` rows whose 03c job was pruned are dropped by the same 03c retention call (7 days).

---

## §6. Commands, events and queries

All commands: `POST /commands/<kebab-name>`, staff session (`sessionKind: 'device'`) unless stated; **not queueable** (G0-1: money, PIN-capable); scope checks last (C8); idempotent by `command_id` (D32; a retransmit returns the stored response); statement counts exclude 00's fixed envelope. Every money result carries the recomputed totals so the screen never computes its own (§8). `bill_ref` in payloads = `{ bill_id }` or `{ seating_id }` or `{ order_id }` (exactly one; an `order_id` resolves to its seating or its `table_key`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-10 + S-9, owner R2); a reference to a bill not yet created creates it, B1).

**Common bill-open step ("ensure bill"),** used by every bill command: resolve `bill_ref` → `openBillOf` (1) → none: read the seating / order (`readOrder` / `seatings`, 1), refuse a seating that is not open (`409 TABLE_NOT_OPEN`) or an order that is cancelled (`409 BILL_NOT_OPEN { state: 'cancelled' }`), a takeaway / parking order not yet sent cannot exist (B16) → append `BillOpened` (with `prices_include_tax` = the mode in force now, H9-9, and the rounding rule in force now, H16-2) + `ChargeApplied { source: 'rule', rate_bp }` (`rate_bp` = `service_charge_bp[order_type]`; 0 → no `ChargeApplied`).

### Batch 1 — the bill (STOP for owner review after this batch)

#### 6.1 `GET /queries/bill?seating_id|order_id|bill_id`

- **Permission:** `take-payment` (its scope: `own_tables` → the caller holds the table / placed the order; `my_floors` → the table's floor) **or** `take-order` (read-only view for the order taker's check bill; ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-20): a payment's `reference` text only to `take-payment` holders whose scope covers the bill, everyone else gets `has_reference`). `403 PERMISSION_DENIED { reason: 'outside_scope' }` last.
- **Result:** `{ bill_id | null, state, scope, order_type, identifier, orders: [order_id], lines: [{ order_item_id, order_id, name, options, units, base_minor, tax_profile_id, tags: ['comp' | 'pending_void' | 'weight_needed' | 'tax_unresolved'] }], service_charge: { rate_bp, removed }, discount | null, splits: [{ split_no, state, plan, payments: [{ payment_id, mode_id, mode_name, direction, amount_minor, tendered_minor, change_minor, reference (from the hub-only `payment_references`, v0.3 H1-20), taken_by_name }], totals: { cash: Totals, digital: Totals, as_paid: Totals }, balance_minor, invoice: { invoice_ref, fiscal_number | null, fiscal_state } | null }], totals_default: Totals, buyer: { required, threshold_minor, waived }, blocks: [{ reason, ids }], open_requests: [{ kind, alert_id }], prints: [{ job_id, doc_kind, state, printer_name, last_error }], check_printed_at, currency: { code, exponent }, prices_include_tax, as_of_seq }` with `Totals = { subtotal_minor, discount_minor, service_charge_minor, taxes: [{ tax_profile_id, tax_code, label, payment_class, rate_bp, base_minor, tax_minor }], rounding_minor, total_minor }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1, J-2): one tax row per (profile, tax code, class); currency and price mode for the client's formatting). Never a buyer tax ID. `blocks` lists every §3.3 rule that would refuse a close now.
- **Statements:** ≤ 9, fixed: `openBillOf` (1), orders of the bill (`seating_orders` or `bill_orders`, 1), `orderLines` (1), `pendingVoids` (1), splits + payments (1 join), invoices of the bill (1), `alerts.listOpen` for the bill's 05 kinds (1), `bill_print_jobs` + `documentJobsStatus` (2). Config and directory in memory. Pure `computeBill`.

#### 6.2 `GET /queries/open-bills?floor_id?`

- **Permission:** `take-payment` (rows filtered by its scope).
- **Result:** `{ bills: [{ bill_id | null, seating_id | null, order_id | null, identifier, order_type, state, total_default_minor, paid_minor, check_printed_at, opened_at, open_minutes, flagged: open_minutes > open_bill_flag_minutes, partly_paid, has_open_request }], as_of_seq }` — ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-10, H2-25): every non-closed `bills` row (not absorbed) whatever its seating's state, plus every open seating with an order that is neither billed nor cancelled ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-21) and every `bill_key` (takeaway / parking) with an unbilled, uncancelled order, one row per key, whether or not a bill row exists yet (projected rows have `bill_id: null`); ordered by `check_printed_at` desc nulls last, then `opened_at`. Bounded by the branch's open tables and orders (≤ 300), returned whole.
- **Statements:** 3 (open seatings ⋈ seating_orders ⋈ orders ⋈ per-profile line sums in one aggregate; unbilled non-table orders by `bill_key` with per-profile sums (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2): was per class); bills ⋈ payments sums). ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-18): the per-profile sums feed the pure `computeBill` in memory (half-up per tax of each profile's list, capped discount spread), so the list total equals the bill view's except pending voids, which the list does not subtract (shown as "before pending voids"). ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-9): `check_printed_at` = max(`bills.check_printed_at`, 02a `seatings.bill_requested_at`).

#### 6.3 `PrintCheckBill` — `{ bill_ref, printer_id? }`

- **Permission** ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-7): registry `take-order` (every Cashier and Order taker holds it by default), scope covering the table / order.
- **Script:** Zod → ensure bill → bill not CLOSED (`409 BILL_NOT_OPEN`) → lines + pending voids (2) → `computeBill` at the default class → printer: payload `printer_id` (must be active, `404 PRINTER_NOT_FOUND`) else `billPrinterFor` (1) → `buildCheckBillDoc` (banner `PROVISIONAL - NOT A TAX INVOICE`, no number, no QR, "total if paid in cash"; plus one line per digital rate when it differs) → `printDocument` (2) + `bill_print_jobs` (1) → append `CheckBillPrinted { bill_id, total_default_minor, line_count, printer_id | null, job_id | null, source: 'manual' }`. No printer → event with nulls, result `{ printed: false }`.
- **Result:** `{ bill_id, job_id | null, printer_name | null, totals_default }`. **Statements:** ≤ 9. **Audit:** `check_bill_printed`.

#### 6.4 Fold `BillRequested` → check bill (live only, B17)

05 registers a live-only reaction on 02a's `BillRequested` (03c's job-fold pattern; skipped on rebuild): ensure-bill drafts are **not** possible in a fold, so the reaction prints ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-9) **the `bills` row's bill when one exists** (its `sc_bp` / `sc_removed`, discount, splits; a removed service charge is never printed back, M-3), else the **projected** bill (same `computeBill`, reads `orderLines` + `pendingVoids` in the transaction after `orders` and `tables` folded) at `billPrinterFor({ zone_id, floor_id })` of the seating's primary table and records the job in `bill_print_jobs` (`bill_id` = the bill if it exists, else NULL with `ref_id = seating_id`). The audit row is 02a's `BillRequested` mapped by 05 as `bill_requested` (§6.30). No 01d notice. A replayed queued `RequestBill` prints at replay time. **Statements:** ≤ 6 per `BillRequested`.

#### 6.5 `SetSplits` — `{ bill_ref, mode: 'items' | 'amount' | 'equal' | 'none', splits?: [{ split_no, lines?: [{ order_item_id, units }], share_minor? }], n? }`

- **Permission:** `take-payment` (scope as §6.1).
- **Script:** Zod (`splits` 2–20, keyed by `split_no`; `lines` keyed by `order_item_id` per split; `n` 2–20 for `equal`) → ensure bill → state OPEN or PARTLY_PAID (closed bill → `409 BILL_NOT_OPEN`) → lines + closed splits (2) → validate per §3.8 over the **open remainder** ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6): closed split numbers are kept and may not appear (`422 BILL_SPLIT_INVALID { reason: 'split_closed' }`); item plan: every live unit not in a closed split exactly once (`reason: 'units_missing' | 'units_exceed', order_item_id`), deal whole and option child with its parent (`'deal_whole' | 'option_with_parent'`); amount plan: Σ shares = the open remainder's default-class total (`'shares_sum', expected_minor`); a split with live payments must keep its number (`409 BILL_HAS_PAYMENTS { split_nos }`); `none` = the open remainder as one split (only when at most one open split has payments) → append `BillSplitsSet { bill_id, mode, splits }`.
- **Result:** the bill view's `splits` (recomputed). **Statements:** ≤ 5. **Audit:** `splits_set` (counts only).

#### 6.6 `ApplyDiscount` — `{ bill_ref, kind: 'amount' | 'percent', value: int, reason_code, note? }` (approvable) ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-4: no `split_no`)

- **Permission:** `apply-discount` (`branch`), `approvable: true`.
- **Script:** Zod (`amount` ≥ 1 minor unit; `percent` in basis points 1–10 000; `reason_code` ∈ the branch's discount reasons, `note` required for `other`) → ensure bill → state PARTLY_PAID → `409 BILL_PARTLY_PAID { bill_id }` (the discount must be set before the first split closes, OQ-4) → state not OPEN → `409 BILL_NOT_OPEN` ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-12: this order, so `BILL_PARTLY_PAID` is reachable) → no open `discount` request on the bill (`409 DISCOUNT_PENDING { alert_id }`) → lines (2) → amount ≤ the whole bill's `S` and ≤ `max_discount_pct` of it (`422 DISCOUNT_TOO_LARGE { max_minor }`) → grant `allowed` → append `DiscountApplied { bill_id, kind, value, amount_minor (at apply), reason_code, has_note, note_id (⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-5): the note in `bill_texts`), requester_staff_id, approver_staff_id: null, alert_id: null, replaces }` → grant `requires_approval` → `raiseMany` kind `discount` (`details { amount, reason, identifier }`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-6)), result `{ pending: { alert_id } }`. The `discount` kind's `apply` re-checks in the same order (PARTLY_PAID, then not OPEN), else throws (the answer is refused).
- **Result:** `{ applied: Totals } | { pending }`. **Statements:** ≤ 6 (+ 01d's raise bound). **Audit:** `discount_applied` / `discount_requested`.

#### 6.7 `RemoveDiscount` — `{ bill_id, reason_code, note? }`

- **Permission:** `apply-discount` (`allowed` or `requires_approval` grant both may remove; removal raises the price, never lowers it). Script: state PARTLY_PAID → `409 BILL_PARTLY_PAID` ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-4) → state not OPEN → `409 BILL_NOT_OPEN` ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-12) → discount present ( no discount → `200 { changed: false }`) → `DiscountRemoved`. **Statements:** 2. **Audit:** `discount_removed`.

#### 6.8 `RemoveServiceCharge` — `{ bill_ref, reason_code, note? }` (approvable) and `ApplyServiceCharge` — `{ bill_ref, note? }`

- **Remove:** `remove-service-charge`, approvable. Ensure bill → OPEN / PARTLY_PAID → already removed → `200 { changed: false }` → `allowed` → `ChargeRemoved { bill_id, charge: 'service', reason_code, has_note, note_id, requester_staff_id, approver_staff_id, alert_id }`; `requires_approval` → kind `remove_service_charge`. **Never** re-applied by any read, print, split, merge or reopen (B8; tests 18, 19).
- **Apply (re-add):** `remove-service-charge` `allowed` (the same right decides both ways) → `ChargeApplied { source: 'manual', rate_bp: service_charge_bp[order_type] }`; already applied → `{ changed: false }`. **Statements:** ≤ 3 each. **Audit:** `service_charge_removed` / `service_charge_applied`.

#### 6.9 `AddPayment` — `{ bill_ref, split_no, mode_id, direction: 'in' | 'out', amount_minor, tendered_minor?, reference? }`

- **Permission:** `take-payment` (scope as §6.1).
- **Script:** Zod (`amount_minor` 1 … 10¹² and ≤ `Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER` (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-13): scaled for any currency); `reference` 1–60, never echoed in an error; `tendered_minor` only on a cash `in`, else `400 VALIDATION_FAILED { path: 'payload.tendered_minor' }`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-28)) → ensure bill → split open (`409 BILL_NOT_OPEN`) → mode active in settings (`404 PAYMENT_MODE_NOT_FOUND`) → the branch's adapter ≠ `none`, the bill's order type is fiscalized and the mode has no `authority_codes` entry for **that adapter** → `422 PAYMENT_MODE_NOT_FISCAL { mode_id, adapter_key }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-4), ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-3): non-fiscalized order types take any mode; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-5): per the branch's adapter, was "no `pra_payment_mode`") → reference rule (`422 PAYMENT_REFERENCE_REQUIRED`) → money shift (B11; state `open`; a counted shift is already closed, owner Q-2): the device's open drawer shift, else the caller's own open shift (an order taker's shift has no drawer, Q9) → none → `409 SHIFT_NOT_OPEN { reason: 'no_shift' }` → lines, pending voids, payments of the split (3) → recompute the split with this payment included (§3.5, §3.6) → an `in` on a split whose balance is ≤ 0 → `422 PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_BALANCE { balance_minor: 0 }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.3 (H17-1)) → an `in` `amount_minor` above **its due field** → `422 PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_BALANCE { balance_minor: <that due> }` ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-2), ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-1, H16-3) (⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25): every `in` ≤ its due field (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26), H15-6: digital ≤ `card_due`, cash ≤ `cash_due`); under `applies_to: 'cash'` a cash amount off the step → `422 PAYMENT_CASH_STEP { step_minor }`, §3.5 (7); v0.7.2–v0.7.5 per-mode dues removed); `out` only when the balance is negative and `amount ≤ −balance` (`422 PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_BALANCE`; ⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25): **every** `out` ≤ its type's refund due — per type, up to what that type paid on the split ("jitna liya utna wapas"); a type that paid nothing → `422 PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_BALANCE { balance_minor: 0 }`; under `applies_to: 'cash'` a cash `out` is a step multiple (`PAYMENT_CASH_STEP`), §3.5 (7)) → append `PaymentAdded { payment_id (ULID), bill_id, split_no, mode_id, payment_class, direction, amount_minor, tendered_minor, change_minor (= tendered − amount, cash only), has_reference, taken_by_staff_id: caller, shift_id }` + `payment_references` row when a reference was given (1) ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-20); ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-3): an `out` that leaves a reopened, all-cancelled bill with every split balance 0 also appends `BillClosedEmpty`.
- **Result:** the split's `totals.as_paid`, `balance_minor`, `{ card_due_minor, cash_due_minor, card_refund_due_minor, cash_refund_due_minor, rounding_minor }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25): the four dues exactly as §3.5 (7) defines them (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26), H15-5, H15-6); `rounding_minor` = `R`, the untaxed adjustment) (~~v0.7.2–v0.7.6 per-mode dues~~ superseded by §25), `change_due_minor` (if cash tendered above), so the screen shows the recomputed tax at once (M-7). **Statements:** ≤ 7. **Audit:** `payment_added` (mode, amount, shift; no names).

#### ~~6.9a `DeclareClassShares`~~ ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26)

Removed with `pro_rata` (out of v1.0; §3.6, §12.7). The command, its event `SplitClassSharesDeclared` and its errors return in v1.1 if an authority requires pro rata (A6).

#### 6.10 `RemovePayment` — `{ payment_id, reason_code: 'wrong_mode' | 'wrong_amount' | 'customer_changed' | 'other', note? }`

- **Permission:** `take-payment`; removing a payment taken by **someone else** also needs `close-shift-for-others` (`403 PERMISSION_DENIED { reason: 'not_own_payment' }`). Payment exists (`404 PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND`), not removed, its split open (`409 BILL_NOT_OPEN`) → its shift OPEN, else `409 SHIFT_NOT_OPEN { reason: 'payment_shift_closed' }` ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-9, owner R4): a payment on a CLOSED shift (counting closes it, owner Q-2; e.g. a reopened earlier bill) is never removed; money back is an `AddPayment { direction: 'out' }` on the actor's own money shift today → `PaymentRemoved { payment_id, bill_id, split_no, reason_code, has_note }`. **Statements:** 2. **Audit:** `payment_removed`.

#### 6.11 `WaiveBuyerId` — `{ bill_id, note? }` (approvable)

- **Permission:** `close-without-buyer-id`, approvable. Bill OPEN / PARTLY_PAID; the whole bill's net above the threshold (else `200 { changed: false, reason: 'not_required' }`); already waived → `{ changed: false }` → `allowed` → `BuyerIdWaived { bill_id, has_note, note_id, requester_staff_id, approver_staff_id: null, alert_id: null }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.5.1 (H4-4)); `requires_approval` → kind `close_without_buyer_id`. Holds for every later split of the bill and survives a reopen (the customer is the same). **Statements:** ≤ 4. **Audit:** `buyer_id_waived`.

#### 6.12 `CloseSplit` — `{ bill_ref, split_no, expected_total_minor, buyer?: { name: string 1–60, tax_id: { type: string, value: string 1–40 } } }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-4): was `id_number`) (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-11): `bill_ref` with ensure bill, so a zero-total bill closes with no payment)

- **Permission:** `take-payment` (scope as §6.1). Not approvable (the money-risky parts are approved before: discount, waiver).
- **Validation:** ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-4): `tax_id.type` ∈ the jurisdiction's `accepted_types` and `tax_id.value`, after removing that type's `strip` characters, matches its `pattern` (Pakistan seed: `CNIC` 13 digits, dashes allowed, stored without; `NTN` 7–9 alphanumerics) → else `400 VALIDATION_FAILED { path: 'payload.buyer.tax_id.value' }` (or `.type`), message without the value. The handler never logs the payload.
- **Script:**
  1. Zod → `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` (00) → bill + split + payments + invoices of the bill (2).
  2. Orders of the bill (1) → `orderLines` (1) → `pendingVoids` (1) → `alerts.listOpen({ kind: 'discount', subject: bill })` and 02b `comp` requests of the orders (2) → dine-in: the seating's state and other open bills (1, for the `BillClosed` rule) ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-28).
  3. §3.3 rules 1–9 in order (first failure throws; nothing written); ~~v0.6.2 `pro_rata` class check~~ (v0.8.1 §26: removed); ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-3): on a fiscalized split, every live payment's mode has a code for the branch's adapter (read at close; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-5)) → else `422 PAYMENT_MODE_NOT_FISCAL { mode_id, adapter_key }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-8): one status; remove it, or pay back and re-take). ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-3): when every order of the bill is cancelled and every split's balance is 0, the command appends **`BillClosedEmpty`** (+ 02a fold, open-request closes as a last close) and **no** `InvoiceIssued`, outbox row or receipt; result `{ bill_state: 'closed', empty: true }`.
  4. `computeSplit` with the split's payments → totals, tax rows, rounding; change = Σ the split's cash payments' `change_minor` (each already on its own shift) ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-2, H1-3).
  5. Number: `SELECT COALESCE(MAX(number), 0) + 1 FROM invoices WHERE series = 'sale'` (1); `invoice_ref = invoice_prefix + 'S' + zero-padded number` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-1); a credit uses `'C'`); unique over both series (`invoices_ref`).
  6. Drafts: `InvoiceIssued { invoice_id, series: 'sale', number, invoice_ref, bill_id, split_no, business_date, issued_at, order_type, jurisdiction_id, currency_code, prices_include_tax, lines: [{ order_item_id, item_id, name, units, unit_price_minor, base_minor, discount_minor, tax_profile_id, fiscal_category (⚠️ v0.7.2 H9-4), taxes: [{ tax_code, payment_class, rate_bp, base_minor, tax_minor }] }] (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-4), ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2): per line, each (profile, tax) row's tax is spread over the profile's lines by line net with `apportionMinor`, Σ per tax = the invoice row), service_charge: { amount_minor, tax_profile_id, taxes: [{ tax_code, payment_class, rate_bp, base_minor, tax_minor }] } (⚠️ v0.7.3 H10-8), subtotal_minor, discount_minor, service_charge_minor, taxes: [{ tax_profile_id, tax_code, label, seq, compound, payment_class, rate_bp, base_minor, tax_minor, rate_ref }], rounding_minor, total_minor, payment_class, mixed_rule, payment_ids, change_minor, discount_line_ids (string[] on the bill's first close, else `null`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-8)), fiscal: { adapter_key, pos_id, required, capabilities, credit_support, rounding (H16-2), receipt: { fiscal_number_label, tax_reg_labels, show_qr, qr_url_template, buyer_id_on_receipt }, seller_registrations: [{ code, number }], payment_codes: [{ payment_id, authority_code \| null }] } (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-6): was `authority`; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-6): the **jurisdiction snapshot** in force at issue, copied into `invoices.doc_json`; a credit copies its original's; credits, credit notes and reprints read it, never today's snapshot), has_buyer_id, buyer_id_refused, closed_by }`; the invoice's `lines` carry each line's invoiced units and share, folded into `closed_split_units` (B15) ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6); the first close freezes the discount amount and lists `discount_line_ids` (the bill's lines at that moment, folded into `bill_discount_lines`, Q-1) (B7); when this is the **last open split**: 02b `OrdersBilled { order_ids, bill_id }` (stream `order`, first order) and, for dine-in when the seating is not `CLOSED` and holds no other open bill (⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-10 + S-9, owner R2): was "first full close only"), 02a `BillClosed { seating_id }` (stream `table`, primary table); `AlertClosed` drafts (`obsolete`, note `bill_closed`) for open `comp` **and `remove_comp`** (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-2)) requests on the **units this split invoices** at every split close ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-23), and at the last close for the remaining `comp` requests on the bill's lines (02b To 05) and any open 05 request on the bill.
  7. Append (00) → projections fold (billing: invoice, outbox row; shifts: takings, change; 02b `billed_at`; 02a NEEDS_CLEANING) → `invoice_buyers` insert when `buyer` is present (1) → receipt: `billPrinterFor({ device_id })` (1), `buildReceiptDoc` (fiscal block = "FISCAL NUMBER PENDING" when `required`, B21, its fields from the jurisdiction's `receipt_fiscal`; buyer name and the **full** buyer tax ID (with its type label) on this customer's receipt only, when the jurisdiction's `receipt_fiscal.buyer_id_on_receipt` is true (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-5, Q-1, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT)); ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-2, Q-2): the `Doc` carries the buyer block as a placeholder `{ fill: 'buyer_id', ref: invoice_id, last4 }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-7)), filled at send by 03c's worker through 05's filler (row P-5), so `print_jobs.payload` stores the last 4 only; drawer block when a cash payment exists) → `printDocument` (2) + `bill_print_jobs` (1).
  8. COMMIT → respond → WS (`InvoiceIssued` to the `bill`-stream audience of §6.30, ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-6); `BillClosed` per 02a).
- **Result:** `{ invoice_id, invoice_ref, total_minor, change_minor, bill_state, receipt: { job_id | null, printer_name | null }, fiscal_state: 'pending' | 'not_required' }`.
- **Statements:** ≤ 18, fixed (no per-line or per-payment statement). Not an engine command (no `EngineState` change) — but it appends events in a transaction with folds: COMMIT-failure test asserts no outbox row, no print job, no buyer row (§10 #36).
- **Audit:** `invoice_issued` (number, total, class, has_buyer_id only).

#### 6.13 `ReopenBill` — `{ bill_id, reason_code ∈ BILL_REOPEN_REASONS, note? }` (approvable)

- **Permission:** `reopen-bill` (`branch`), approvable.
- **Step order** ⚠️ Changed in v0.5.1 (H4-1): (1) read the bill, its invoices and **its order set** (`bill_orders`, or for PARTLY_PAID the bill's current orders); (2) read the seating / key state and the other bills and unbilled orders on it; (3) decide the mode (§3.10) with the set from (1) excluded; (4) build the drafts, `BillReopened { order_ids }` first, so its fold fills `bill_orders` before any later read in the same transaction.
- **Script:** Zod → bill CLOSED or PARTLY_PAID (`409 BILL_NOT_OPEN { state }`) → an open `reopen_bill` request on the bill (`alerts.listOpen`, 1) → `409 BILL_REOPEN_PENDING { alert_id }` → invoices and the bill's order set (2) → ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1, J-6): a sale invoice whose outbox row is `pending` / `acked` and whose **snapshot** (H9-6) has `capabilities.credit = false` or `credit_support = 'none'` → `409 BILL_REOPEN_NOT_SUPPORTED { invoice_id }` (never for the Pakistan seed or `none`; the `reopen_bill` `apply` re-checks it on approve) → the seating's state (dine-in), or the `bill_key`'s other bills (takeaway / parking), and the unbilled orders outside the set (1) ⚠️ Changed in v0.5.2 (H5-4) → mode per §3.10 ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-10 + S-9, owner R2) → `allowed` → drafts per §3.10 (+ 02a `SeatingReopened { seating_id, bill_id }` in full-edit mode): `BillReopened { bill_id, order_ids (the bill's current order set; folded into `bill_orders` with `INSERT OR IGNORE`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-2)), reason_code, has_note, note_id, requester_staff_id, approver_staff_id: null, alert_id: null }`, one `InvoiceCredited { invoice_id (new), alert_id (null on the direct path, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-1)), series: 'credit', number (MAX+1 of credit, 1), invoice_ref (`prefix + 'C' + number`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-1)), ref_invoice_id, ref_invoice_ref, bill_id, split_no, business_date (today), …the original's amounts, its `jurisdiction_id`, `currency_code`, `prices_include_tax`, lines, tax rows and `fiscal` snapshot (H9-6) copied (never re-read from today's rates, B5), fiscal }` per current sale invoice, `OrdersUnbilled` when CLOSED → `requires_approval` → kind `reopen_bill` (values `{ bill_id, invoice_ids, note_id, has_note }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.5.1 (H4-4)) — the current sale invoices at raise; on **approve** `apply` throws, so the answer is refused, when they differ at answer time (⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-9); a reject always succeeds), ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-17); every `apply` draft carries the request's `alert_id`, H2-1; `apply` builds the same drafts read-only, numbers from the answer transaction's read view). On both paths the credit note is printed by the live-only fold of `InvoiceCredited` (§3.16), never by the command, so it prints once.
- **Result:** `{ reopened: { mode: 'full' | 'corrections', credits: [{ invoice_ref, total_minor }] } } | { pending: { alert_id } }` ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-26). **Statements:** ≤ 8. **Audit:** `bill_reopened` (with the mode), `invoice_credited`.

#### 6.14 `RemoveComp` — `{ order_item_id, units?, reason_code ∈ COMP_REMOVE_REASONS, note? }` (approvable)

- **Permission:** `remove-comp` (`branch`), approvable.
- **Script:** `readOrder` (1) → order not billed (`409 ORDER_BILLED`), line comped (`409 LINE_NOT_COMPED { order_item_id }`), `units ≤ comped_units` (`400`) → line guard list (02b, 1: a void pending on it → `409 VOID_PENDING`; units in a closed split → `409 LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT`) ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6) → `planCompRemoval` (02b) → `allowed` → `LineCompRemoved { …, approver_staff_id: null, alert_id: null }` (02b event, 05 writes it); `requires_approval` → kind `remove_comp` (dedupe `remove_comp:<order_item_id>`).
- **Result:** `{ removed: { units, comp_minor } } | { pending }`. **Statements:** ≤ 4. **Audit:** `comp_removed`.

### Batch 2 — shifts, petty cash, drawer

#### 6.15 `OpenShift` — `{ float_minor, as_drawer: boolean }`

- **Permission:** `manage-own-shift`.
- **Script:** Zod (`float_minor` 0 … 10¹², ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-13)) → business day open (`409 BUSINESS_DAY_NOT_OPEN`, 01a's code) → caller has no open shift (`409 SHIFT_ALREADY_OPEN { shift_id }`) → `as_drawer`: the device is a `cashier_kiosk` or a tablet with a printer route (else `422 DRAWER_DEVICE_INVALID { device_id }`), and no open shift holds this device (`409 DRAWER_IN_USE { shift_id, staff_name }`) → `ShiftOpened { shift_id, staff_id, drawer_device_id | null, float_minor, business_date }` → drawer kick (`printDocument` with `{ drawer: true }` on the device route) when `float_minor > 0`.
- **Result:** `{ shift_id, opened_at }`. **Statements:** ≤ 5. **Audit:** `shift_opened`.

#### 6.16 `GET /queries/shift?shift_id?`

- **Permission** ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-7): registry `manage-own-shift`; another person's shift additionally needs `close-shift-for-others`, checked in the handler with `ctx.directory.resolve` (`403 PERMISSION_DENIED { permission: 'close-shift-for-others' }`).
- **Result:** `{ shift_id, staff_name, state, is_drawer, opened_at, float_minor, takings: [{ mode_id, mode_name, payment_class, in_minor, out_minor, change_minor, by: [{ taken_by_name, in_minor }] }], petty: [{ entry_id, kind, amount_minor, category_name, paid_to (read from the hub-only `bill_texts`, never from an event, ⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-5)), advance_staff_name, settled }], open_advances_minor, handovers: [...], counted_minor | null, expected_minor | null, variance_minor | null }`. **Blind rule (B23):** `expected_minor` and `variance_minor` are `null` unless `counted_at` is set (a handover row shows the expected of its own count only); card / online takings **are** shown (they are checked against the terminal, story 7 AC5); the cash line shows only the count of cash payments, never their sum, before the count. **Statements:** 4.

#### 6.17 `CountShift` — `{ shift_id, counted_minor, note? }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT): counts **and closes**)

- **Permission** ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-7): registry `manage-own-shift`; another person's shift additionally needs `close-shift-for-others`, checked in the handler with `ctx.directory.resolve` (`403 PERMISSION_DENIED { permission: 'close-shift-for-others' }`).
- **Script:** shift OPEN (`409 SHIFT_NOT_OPEN { reason: 'not_open' }`) → `ShiftCounted { shift_id, counted_minor, expected_minor, variance_minor, by_staff_id }` (expected from the running fold) **+ `ShiftClosed { shift_id, closed_by, has_note, note_id, alert_id: null, approver_staff_id: null }`** in the same transaction (the drawer is free at once) → `|variance| > variance_threshold_minor` → the fold sets `variance_state = 'pending'` and `raiseMany` kind `shift_variance` (`details { amount: variance_minor }`, approvers §3.14; dedupe `variance:<shift_id>`); it is a sign-off only ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT). ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-6): the shift's open `petty_cash` requests are closed `obsolete` (note `shift_closed`) in the same transaction and listed in the result.
- **Result:** `{ shift_id, closed: true, expected_minor, variance_minor, sign_off: { alert_id } | null }` — the first time the server reveals expected. **Statements:** ≤ 4 (+ 01d's raise bound). **Audit:** `shift_counted`, `shift_closed`.

#### 6.18 `CloseShift` — `{ shift_id, skip_count: true, note? }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT): only the no-count close; a counted close is `CountShift`)

- **Permission** ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-7): registry `manage-own-shift`; another person's shift additionally `close-shift-for-others` (handler); `skip_count` → also `skip-blind-count` (`403 PERMISSION_DENIED { reason: 'skip_not_granted' }`).
- **Script:** shift exists (`404 SHIFT_NOT_FOUND`), OPEN (`409 SHIFT_NOT_OPEN { reason: 'not_open' }`) → `skip-blind-count` granted (`403 PERMISSION_DENIED { reason: 'skip_not_granted' }`) → `ShiftCountSkipped` + `ShiftClosed { shift_id, closed_by, has_note, note_id, alert_id: null, approver_staff_id: null }`. No variance, no request. ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-6): open `petty_cash` requests of the shift closed `obsolete` as in §6.17. Open advances are listed in the result, never blocking. (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT): v0.6's "not counted → `SHIFT_NOT_COUNTED`" and v0.5.2's repeated-close `SHIFT_VARIANCE_PENDING` (H5-5) are gone: a counted shift is already closed.)
- **Result:** `{ closed: { shift_id } } | { pending }`. **Statements:** ≤ 5. **Audit:** `shift_closed` / `shift_count_skipped`.

#### 6.19 `HandoverCount` — `{ shift_id, counted_minor }`

- **Permission** ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-7): registry `manage-own-shift`; another person's drawer shift additionally `close-shift-for-others` (handler). Drawer shift OPEN → `HandoverCounted { shift_id, counted_minor, expected_minor, variance_minor, by_staff_id }`; the shift stays OPEN; the result reveals expected (it is a count). **Statements:** 2. **Audit:** `handover_counted`.

#### 6.20 `RecordPettyCash` — `{ kind, amount_minor, category_id, paid_to?, advance_staff_id?, note? }` (approvable) and `SettlePettyAdvance` — `{ entry_id, spent_minor, returned_minor, note? }`

- **Record:** `record-petty-cash`, approvable. Caller's money shift (B11; cash; state `open`) → else `409 SHIFT_NOT_OPEN` → category active (`422 CONFIG_REFERENCE_INVALID { field: 'category_id', reason: 'not_found' }`) → advance needs `advance_staff_id` in the directory (`422 … 'advance_staff_id'`) (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-15): no check against the shift's expected cash — a refusal would let a cashier binary-search it; an overdraw shows as variance at the count) → grant `allowed` and `amount ≤ petty_cash_limit_minor` → `PettyCashAdvanced` / `PettyCashOut` / `PettyCashIn { entry_id, shift_id, amount_minor, category_id, has_paid_to, advance_staff_id, has_note, requester_staff_id, approver_staff_id: null, alert_id: null }` + drawer kick → else kind `petty_cash` (values carry the entry **without text** — `entry_id`, amounts, ids, `has_note` / `has_paid_to`; the texts go to `bill_texts` under `entry_id` at raise, ⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-5); `apply` returns the event; the drawer kick on approval is the requester's next `OpenCashDrawer`, not automatic).
- **Settle:** `record-petty-cash` `allowed`. Advance open (`404 PETTY_CASH_NOT_FOUND`, `409 PETTY_ADVANCE_SETTLED`), `spent + returned = amount` (`422 PETTY_SETTLE_MISMATCH { amount_minor }`) → caller's money shift (the returned cash lands there) → `PettyCashSettled { entry_id, shift_id, spent_minor, returned_minor, has_note, note_id }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.5.1 (H4-4): its own `note_id` ULID, so it never collides with the advance's `(entry_id, 'note')` row).
- **Statements:** ≤ 4 each. **Audit:** `petty_cash` (kind, amount, category; `paid_to` and names never in the audit detail).

#### 6.21 `OpenCashDrawer` — `{ reason_code: 'change_for_customer' | 'check_cash' | 'other', note? }`

- **Permission:** `open-cash-drawer`. The device is a drawer of a shift in state `open` (`409 SHIFT_NOT_OPEN { reason: 'no_drawer_shift' }`) → `DrawerOpened { shift_id, device_id, reason_code, has_note, note_id }` → `printDocument` (`{ drawer: true }`). **Statements:** 4. **Audit:** `drawer_opened`.

### Batch 3 — day close, reports, printing, fiscal

#### 6.22 `PrintBillDocument` — `{ doc: 'receipt' | 'credit_note', ref_id, printer_id? }` and `PrintReport` — `{ doc: 'x_report' | 'z_report', ref_id?, printer_id? }` ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-7: split)

- **Permission:** `PrintBillDocument` → `take-payment`; `PrintReport` → `view-reports` (one registry permission each, ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-7)).
- **Script:** the invoice (`404 INVOICE_NOT_FOUND`) / the Z (`404 Z_REPORT_NOT_FOUND`) / the open day's X from the projections (≤ 6) → builder (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-6): labels, QR template and registrations from the invoice's `fiscal` snapshot, never today's; a receipt reprint is marked `REPRINT`, shows the fiscal number + QR once `FiscalNumberAssigned` exists, else "FISCAL NUMBER PENDING"; buyer from `invoice_buyers`, the full buyer tax ID on that invoice's reprint only (rebuilt from `invoice_buyers` through the same filler, never from the print queue, ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-2, Q-2); a credit note prints the last 4, Q-3), ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-5, Q-1, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT)) → printer (payload, else route) → `printDocument` + `bill_print_jobs` → no event (a reprint changes no money; the audit of reprints is 03c's `document` job list). **Statements:** ≤ 9.

#### 6.23 `RetargetBillPrint` — `{ job_id, printer_id }`

- **Permission:** `take-order` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-7)). `bill_print_jobs` row exists (`404 PRINT_JOB_NOT_FOUND`) → `retargetDocumentJob` (03c row P-2; `409 PRINT_JOB_NOT_PENDING` when printed / being sent / cancelled; `404 PRINTER_NOT_FOUND`). No event. **Statements:** 3. Result: the job's new status.

#### 6.24 `CarryOverOrders` — `{ order_ids: string[1..40], reason_code ∈ CARRY_OVER_REASONS, note? }`

- ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-9, owner R4): a carried bill **may** already have payments (an open split or a partly paid bill); the close is allowed and the X / Z show them as **carried forward** (and the next day's as **brought forward**, §6.26, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 H2-3).
- **Permission:** `close-business-day`. Each order unbilled and not cancelled (`409 ORDER_BILLED` / `400` per index) → `OrdersCarriedOver { order_ids, business_date, reason_code, has_note, note_id }` (`hub` stream). They survive this day's close only. **Statements:** 2. **Audit:** `orders_carried_over`.

#### 6.25 Day close — 01a `CloseBusinessDay` with 05's guard and appender (rows A-3, A-4, A-5)

- **05 guard** (after 04's; ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT): a pending variance sign-off never blocks — it is not an open shift and the appender does not close its request; the Z lists it as "pending sign-off"): open shifts of any day (1, `shifts_open_staff`) → `409 DAY_CLOSE_BLOCKED { reason: 'shift_open', shifts, count }`; unbilled, uncancelled, not carried orders (1) → `{ reason: 'unbilled_orders', orders, count }` (≤ 40 listed, `count` via `COUNT(*) OVER ()`).
- **05 appender:** open 05 requests + 02b `comp` requests (`alerts.listOpen`, 1) → `AlertClosed { outcome: 'obsolete', note: 'day_closed' }` drafts (01d `close` helper); Z number = MAX + 1 (1); `buildReport` over the day (§6.26, ≤ 6) → `ZReportIssued { business_date, z_no, report }` (`hub` stream). The live-only fold prints it at the `report` route. ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-5): `ZReportIssued.report` carries **staff ids only** (`staff_id`, `taken_by_staff_id`, `approver_staff_id`, `advance_staff_id`); names are resolved at render (`buildReportDoc`, the X / Z queries) from `staff_names`, never stored in the event.
- **Bound:** 05 adds ≤ 10 statements to the close. **Engine:** the command already has its COMMIT-failure test; 05 adds one asserting no `ZReportIssued`, no `z_reports` row and no print job after a failed COMMIT (§10 #63).

#### 6.26 `GET /queries/x-report` and `GET /queries/z-reports?business_date?|cursor&limit`

- **Permission:** `view-reports`.
- **Report content (B26, `buildReport`):** ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (owner Q-1): the report states the tenant's `currency_code` (`ZReportIssued.report.currency_code`); sales: sale invoices count, gross (Σ subtotal), discounts (count, value, by reason, requester / approver / method from the audit fold), service charge, net, **tax by tax code, profile and payment class** with rate (⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25): on exact amounts; **"rounding" is its own line**, outside every tax row and outside payments by mode) (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2): one line per `invoice_taxes` key; a one-tax profile prints as v0.6.2's "tax by class" line), rounding, total; credits (count, value, by reason) and **net = sales − credits**; payments by mode (in, out, change); comps (value), voided comps (`comp_voided_minor`, 02b O21), comp removals; voids by disposition (wastage / staff meal / moved / null, count and value, 04 G-8, from `ItemVoided`); reopens (count, value, requester, approver); petty cash by category and kind, open advances (who, amount, age); per shift: staff, float, expected, counted, variance, skipped flag, **variance sign-off state** (`pending sign-off` / `approved` / `disputed`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT)); ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-1): two more lines — **"variance sign-offs"** = every `ShiftVarianceSignedOff` recorded in the report's day, any shift date (shift date, staff, amount, outcome, approver, time; `shift_signoffs_day`), and **"pending sign-offs"** = every shift of any date with `variance_state = 'pending'` at issue (date, staff, amount; `shifts_signoff_pending`); X shows both live; both outside takings (invariant unchanged); X bound +1 statement; takings by taker (Q10); **full refunds** (`BillClosedEmpty`: count and the credit value, already inside "credits", ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-6, Q-3, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT)); fiscal: invoices pending / acked / rejected / not required; bills count, average bill, open bills (X only, with "open too long" flags, M-8); carried-over orders (Z of the day they are carried into lists them as "carried in"); **"brought forward"** and **"carried forward"** (payments on open bills) lines by mode and shift ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-9, owner R4), ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-3). **X** = the same over the open day, the shifts' expected cash **hidden** for shifts not counted (B23).
- **Statements:** X ≤ 8 fixed (invoices + taxes of the day, payments by mode, shifts + takings, petty, audit rows for discounts / reopens / voids of the day, outbox counts, open bills); Z list: 1 (cursor, F §6.3).
- **Invariant (property test)** ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-3), ⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-3): **brought forward** = live payments on **splits that are not closed** at the day's open (incl. a reopened bill's splits and a carried bill's open splits); **carried forward** = the same at the close. Payments of a closed split are in its invoice and never counted again. **Σ the day's takings (`in` − `out`, all modes) = Z net (Σ sale − Σ credit totals of the day) + carried forward − brought forward.** The Z prints both lines by mode. A payment never changes after its shift is counted.

#### 6.27 `GET /queries/fiscal-status`

- **Permission:** `take-payment` or `view-reports`.
- **Result:** `{ jurisdiction_id, adapter_key, environment, adapter_health: 'ok' | 'failing' | 'unknown', last_success_at, pending_count, oldest_pending_age_ms, rejected_count, catch_up_window_ms, stuck: boolean, token_missing: [{ pos_id, name }], tokens: [{ pos_id, name, set_at }] }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.4 (H11-4): per credential name; replaces `token_missing_pos_ids`) (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1): was `authority`) — never a token, never a buyer (⚠️ Changed in v0.2, OQ-3: `tokens` shows only "set on <date>"). **Statements:** 3.

#### 6.28 `RetryFiscalNow` — `{}`

- **Permission:** `close-business-day`. Sets `next_try_at = now` for `pending` rows (plain statement, not an event; 1) and wakes the worker; a `rejected` row is **not** resent here (it needs the fix the rejection names; a developer task). Result `{ pending_count }`.

#### 6.28a `SetFiscalToken` — `POST /admin/commands/set-fiscal-token`, `{ pos_id, name?, token }` ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-3, new), ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.3 (H10-5: `name`)

- **Permission:** `manage-fiscal-settings` (allowed-only; **Owner only** by default, row B-1 / B-2), `sessionKind: 'admin'` (hub admin page `/admin/fiscal/`, row H-2). Not queueable.
- **Script:** Zod (`pos_id` 1–40 and listed in the branch fiscal settings' `pos_ids`, else `422 CONFIG_REFERENCE_INVALID { field: 'pos_id', reason: 'not_found' }`; `name` 1–40, default `'token'`, one of the `credentialNames` of the branch's adapter or of an adapter recorded on a pending row (else `422 CONFIG_REFERENCE_INVALID { field: 'name', reason: 'not_found' }`); `token` 1–512 printable, the Zod message never echoes it) → `INSERT … ON CONFLICT (pos_id, name) DO UPDATE` into `fiscal_credentials` (replace = a new token; there is no read-back) (1) → append `FiscalTokenSet { pos_id, name, set_at, set_by_staff_id }` (`hub` stream; **no token, no length, no hash**) → the worker picks the new token at its next send.
- **Result:** `{ pos_id, name, set_at }` — never the token. **Statements:** 2. **Audit:** `fiscal_token_changed` (ids and time only).
- **Rules:** no query, response, log line, event, sync payload or cloud row ever carries the token; `processed_commands` stores only the result above; the page field is write-only and never pre-filled; removing a token is not offered in the pilot (replace instead).

#### 6.29 Events (`schema_version` 1, `.strict()`, `@pos/events/src/events/<stream>/`)

| Event | Stream | Payload (besides ids named) | Folded by |
|---|---|---|---|
| `BillOpened` | bill | `{ bill_id, scope, seating_id, bill_key, order_type, prices_include_tax, rounding: { step_minor, mode, applies_to } }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-2)) (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-9): the mode in force at open) (⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-16): `bill_key` non-null for scope `key`) | billing |
| `ChargeApplied` | bill | `{ bill_id, charge: 'service', rate_bp, source: 'rule' \| 'manual', has_note, note_id }` | billing |
| `ChargeRemoved` | bill | `{ bill_id, charge, reason_code, has_note, note_id, requester_staff_id, approver_staff_id, alert_id }` | billing |
| `CheckBillPrinted` | bill | `{ bill_id, total_default_minor, line_count, printer_id, job_id, source }` | billing |
| `BillSplitsSet` | bill | `{ bill_id, mode, splits }` | billing |
| `DiscountApplied` / `DiscountRemoved` | bill | §6.6 / §6.7 | billing |
| `PaymentAdded` / `PaymentRemoved` | bill | §6.9 / §6.10 (`has_reference` only, never the text, ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 H1-20) | billing, shifts |
| ~~`SplitClassSharesDeclared`~~ ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26) | — | removed with `pro_rata` | — |
| `BuyerIdWaived` | bill | `{ bill_id, has_note, note_id, requester_staff_id, approver_staff_id, alert_id }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.5.1 (H4-4)) | billing |
| `InvoiceIssued` | bill | §6.12 step 6 (no buyer tax ID, ever; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1, J-2): `jurisdiction_id`, `currency_code`, `prices_include_tax`, per-line `taxes[]`) | billing, shifts |
| `BillReopened` | bill | §6.13 | billing |
| `InvoiceCredited` | bill | §6.13 (+ `alert_id: string \| null`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-1)) | billing |
| `FiscalNumberAssigned` | bill | `{ invoice_id, adapter_key, pos_id, fiscal_number, qr_payload }` (worker; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-6): was `authority`) | billing |
| `FiscalSubmitRejected` | bill | `{ invoice_id, adapter_key, error_code (≤ 40), http_status }` (worker; no request body; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-6)) | billing |
| `ShiftOpened`, `ShiftCounted`, `ShiftCountSkipped`, `HandoverCounted`, `ShiftClosed` | shift | §6.15–§6.19 | shifts |
| `ShiftVarianceSignedOff` ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT) | shift | `{ shift_id, outcome: 'approved' \| 'disputed', alert_id, approver_staff_id }` | shifts |
| `PettyCashAdvanced`, `PettyCashSettled`, `PettyCashOut`, `PettyCashIn` | shift | §6.20 | shifts |
| `DrawerOpened` | shift | §6.21 | shifts |
| `OrdersCarriedOver`, `ZReportIssued` | hub | §6.24, §6.25 | billing |
| `BillClosedEmpty` ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-6, Q-3 (b)), v0.6.1 (H7-3), ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-5, H8-6, H8-8, H8-10) | bill | **`{ bill_id, seating_id: string \| null, credit_invoice_ids: string[], alert_id?: string }`** — the one payload (every other mention points here); `seating_id` set only under §6.12's `BillClosed` rule (dine-in, seating not CLOSED, no other open bill), else `null`; `alert_id` when the order-cancelled hook runs inside an approval `apply`. Fold: bill CLOSED and **every split `state = 'closed'`, `invoice_id` NULL**, so its payments leave brought / carried forward (their sum is 0). No sale invoice, no `OrdersBilled`, no fiscal row, no receipt. Z: not in bills count / average; **"full refunds"** = bills empty-closed in the day with the value of their credits of any day (each credit is counted in the day it was issued); the `out` payments in takings | billing |
| `FiscalTokenSet` ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-3) | hub | `{ pos_id, name, set_at, set_by_staff_id }` (no token; `name` v0.7.3 H10-5) | billing (audit only) |
| 02b `OrdersBilled`, `OrdersUnbilled`, `LineCompRemoved`; 02a `BillClosed`, `SeatingReopened` (⚠️ Changed in v0.3, R2) | order / table | owned by 02b / 02a (rows Q-2, Q-4), written by 05 | orders, tables |

Every 05 event carries `has_note` / `has_paid_to` instead of free text (⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-22); the text in `bill_texts`). ⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-5): **no payload and no alert `values` carry text**; `bill_texts` is keyed by `subject_id` = the id the command mints and the event carries (`entry_id` for petty cash, `payment_id` for a payment, `note_id` — a ULID minted by the command — for bill-, charge-, discount-, reopen-, drawer- and carry-over notes), written by the command on the direct path and at raise on the approval path; `apply` never reads text. `DiscountApplied`, `DiscountRemoved`, `BillReopened` carry `has_note`, `note_id` likewise. ⚠️ Changed in v0.5.1 (H4-4): **every approvable kind's `values` carry `note_id` and `has_note`** (`discount`, `remove_service_charge`, `reopen_bill`, `remove_comp`, `close_without_buyer_id`, `petty_cash`; `shift_variance` takes `CountShift`'s `note_id` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-9))) and `apply` copies them into its event, so an approved note resolves in `bill_texts`; `BuyerIdWaived`, `LineCompRemoved`, `ShiftClosed` carry `has_note`, `note_id`; `PettyCashSettled` has its own `note_id`. Events written by approval `apply` carry the request's `alert_id` and `approver_staff_id` = `AnswerFacts.answerer_staff_id` (01d contract). Builders in `@pos/events/testing` for each (F §6.7) and one fixture per event.

#### 6.30 Registrations at startup (`apps/hub/src/billing/index.ts`)

**WS audiences** ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-6; `ws/audience.ts:22-25`: an event type with no audience reaches no socket): `registerEventAudience` for every `bill`-stream event → staff sockets holding `take-payment` (scope as §6.1) and cashier kiosks; every `shift`-stream event → the shift's own staff's sockets and holders of `close-shift-for-others` only, with `ShiftCounted` / `HandoverCounted` (which carry expected cash) to those only after the count; `ZReportIssued`, `OrdersCarriedOver` → holders of `view-reports` / `close-business-day`; `FiscalTokenSet` → admin sessions only. ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.3 (H17-4): **no frame carries `expected_minor` or `variance_minor` before the shift's count (B23)**; `ShiftCounted` / `HandoverCounted` go only to the shift's own staff and `close-shift-for-others` holders, after the count; the inputs of expected cash (payments, petty cash, the float) reach the cashier's own socket by design.

**At module load, before any projection is built** ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-8: `registerAuditAction` throws after the `audit` projection seals): `registerAuditAction` for each event in row V-3 and `registerRequestAction` for the seven kinds (map: actor = requester for approved actions, approver from the event, `subject_type 'bill' | 'shift' | 'order' | 'day'`, `amount_minor`, `reason`; detail ≤ 2 KB with ids and counts; **no buyer fields, no reference, no `paid_to`, no notes beyond the reason**); the eight alert kinds (§3.14, `fiscal_outbox_stuck`) at module top level (01d boot rule). **Then:** projections `billing`, `shifts` (after `orders`, `tables`, `audit`); live-only print folds (`BillRequested`, `InvoiceCredited`, `ZReportIssued`); `registerDayCloseGuard`, `registerDayCloseAppender` (rows A-3, A-4); `registerMergeGuard` (row T-1); `registerLineGuard` (row Q-3); `registerFiscalAdapter(noneAdapter)`, `registerFiscalAdapter(praAdapter)` (Appendix P); `startFiscalWorker` via 00 row H-3.

#### 6.31 Example — Table 7, Rs 5,710 of food (one profile with one tax, restaurant service), jurisdiction `PK-PB` (adapter `PRA`), prices exclusive, rounding step Rs 1, `applies_to: 'all'`

1. Waiter taps **Ask for bill** → 02a `BillRequested` → 05 prints the check bill at the first-floor bill printer: subtotal 5,710.00; tax 16 % (cash) 913.60 → total if paid in cash **6,624.00** (rounding +0.40); a line "card / wallet / QR: 5,996.00". Banner `PROVISIONAL - NOT A TAX INVOICE`. `bill_requested` audit row.
2. All by card: the cashier taps Card → the screen shows 5,710 + 285.50 = 5,995.50 → **5,996.00** (+0.50) → `AddPayment` card 5,996.00 → `CloseSplit { expected_total_minor: 599600 }` → `InvoiceIssued` `AC1-S000123` (class digital, tax row 500 bp), `OrdersBilled`, `BillClosed`; outbox row pending; receipt "FISCAL NUMBER PENDING" if the worker has not answered yet; no drawer kick.
3. Mixed instead, Rs 3,000 card + rest cash: `higher_rate` (the `PK-PB` seed) → the whole split at 16 % → 6,624.00, card 3,000 + cash 3,624. (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26): `pro_rata` is out of v1.0, §3.6.)
4. The worker calls the branch adapter's `submitSale` with idempotency key `AC1-S000123` (the PRA mapping is Appendix P §P.2) → `FiscalNumberAssigned`; a reprint shows the fiscal number with the jurisdiction's label and the QR.
