## §7. Cross-cutting (each item with the failure it prevents)

| Item | Rule | Prevents |
|---|---|---|
| Transactions | Every 05 command in one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`; close / reopen / day-close checks inside it (F §6.3) | void approved, weight entered or a second kiosk closing between check and write (F §1 race table) |
| Idempotency | `command_id` (D32) for every command; the fiscal outbox keyed by `invoice_id`, idempotency key = `invoice_ref` (v0.7 J-6) | double invoice on a retransmit; double fiscal post after a crash |
| Gap-free numbers | `MAX + 1` under the single writer + unique index | a gap or duplicate an authority audit would flag |
| Fiscal worker | one in flight; back-off 5 s → 30 s → 2 min → every 5 min; HTTP timeout 10 s; never on a command path; started after listen (row H-3); paused while the hub has no route to the internet (`fetch` fails fast) | close waiting for the internet; a flood of retries on a dead link |
| Stuck alert | §3.15; one alert per branch | an invoice missing the jurisdiction's catch-up window unnoticed |
| Privacy | buyer tax ID (every type, J-4) only in `invoice_buyers`; the full ID prints only on that customer's receipt and its reprints (owner Q-1); no screen, check bill, X / Z shows it; no response returns it; logs carry ids and counts only (B30); `details` / audit / Z never carry names beyond the existing `staff_names` read model, never buyer data, never `paid_to` | a buyer tax ID leaks into the immutable, synced log or the cloud |
| Secrets | tokens only in the hub table `fiscal_credentials`, entered on the hub admin page (B22, §6.28a) ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-3); the adapter reads at send; error texts from the authority are stored as a short code | a token in the repo, a log, an event or the cloud |
| Blind count | `expected_*` fields null before `ShiftCounted`, enforced in the query and command result builders (one function); test per response | cashier sees expected before counting (D21) |
| Rate effective date | issue date = local calendar date of `issued_at` in the hub's time zone (01a S47 rule) for every rate row and every `jurisdiction_rules` row; the bill view shows the rate row's ref | wrong rate on the notification day |
| Performance | close ≤ 18 statements, bill view ≤ 9, X ≤ 8 (fixed, no N+1); no timing assert in the gate (03a AD-32); `test:perf` adds a 200-bill rush close row on the pilot box | close slow at peak (200 bills / rush, R6) |
| Rate limits | none new (LAN, PIN lockout covers PIN answers, 01d) | — |
| Offline | close, payments, shifts, petty cash, day close work on the LAN without internet (G0-1); only the fiscal answer waits | billing stops when the internet drops (E5) |
| Folder | `apps/hub/src/billing/{commands,queries,projections,fiscal,print,kinds,lanes}/`, `packages/domain/src/billing/`, `packages/events/src/events/{bill,shift}/`, `apps/client/src/features/billing/` (F §6.9 `features/cashier` named `billing`); no new top-level folder | — |

---

## §8. Client contract (`apps/client`, staff entry; R5 permission-derived; ui-kit only)

- **Cashier screen** (kiosk / tablet with `take-payment`): open-bills list (§6.2) pulled on focus, on `event` frames of bill / order / table streams and on reconnect; "check bill printed" badge; "open too long" flag; partly-paid badge. No push, no sound for bill requests (Q13).
- **Bill screen:** lines with COMP / PENDING VOID / WEIGHT NEEDED tags; totals from the server only (§6.1) — the client never computes tax; **no tax buttons** (G1-15); the payment-mode pad shows each mode; choosing a mode shows the total at that class before taking it (M-7); ⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25): the pad shows the balance and the **"Rounding adjustment"** line separately from tax; the card and cash dues are shown side by side (§3.5 (7); under cash-only rounding the cash due is rounded down to the coin step, H15-5); a paid split shows no payment due and a negative balance shows only **"refund card <x> / cash <y>"** (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.3 (H17-1)) (per type up to what it paid; a type with 0 is hidden; a full refund shows exactly what each type paid); ~~a `pro_rata` declaration step~~ (v0.8.1 §26: removed); tendered amount entry with change due (M-6; never pre-filled with the total); split editor (items / amount / equal); discount sheet (reason buttons from settings, approval waiting card); service charge row with Remove / Add back; buyer sheet when `buyer.required` (name + a type picker from the jurisdiction's accepted types + the ID value, ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-4); "Customer refuses" → `WaiveBuyerId`); Close button disabled with the server's `blocks` text; `BILL_TOTAL_CHANGED` → refresh and show the new total, never auto-close. Online-only actions show "Needs the hub" offline (G0-1).
- **Print status:** each bill document shows waiting / printed / failed with the printer name; on failed or waiting > 10 s a **Print to** picker lists active printers (`RetargetBillPrint`, B18).
- **Shift:** open with float; close shows card / online totals and asks for the counted cash **before** any expected figure; variance result after the count; "Variance sent for sign-off (pending); the drawer is free" (⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-9)); open advances list; handover count (drawer shifts).
- **Petty cash:** advance / out / in sheet with categories; open advances with Settle.
- **Day close** (staff app, `close-business-day`): blockers list (voids, open shifts, unbilled orders with Carry over), then Close; Z shown and printed. `/admin/stock` keeps its Close day (row A-5).
- **Reports:** X (any time) and Z list (`view-reports`); fiscal status line (pending count, oldest age) on the cashier screen when the adapter is not `none`.
- **Errors:** every §9 code has an `errors.<code>` key (EN + UR, D12); `DAY_CLOSE_BLOCKED` reasons render as lists.
- **i18n:** amounts formatted only in the client (D47) with the bill's `currency.exponent` (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1, row F-6): never a hard-coded 2 decimals); Latin digits in Urdu (R20); no other locale work (§17).
- **Owner app** (01a row A-1 (6)): Billing settings page with the receipt preview (`renderText`) and the fiscal catalog; no secret field.
- **Hub admin `/admin/fiscal/`** ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-3): per `pos_id` "Token: set on <date>" / "not set", a write-only field + Save (`SetFiscalToken`), shown by `manage-fiscal-settings`; the field is cleared after Save and never filled from the server. ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.4 (H11-4): **one write-only field per (`pos_id`, `name`)** for every `credentialNames` entry of the branch's adapter and of any adapter on a pending row (PRA: one "Token" per `pos_id`, as before), each with "set on <date>" / "not set" and its own Save sending `SetFiscalToken { pos_id, name, token }`; same rules as the token (never pre-filled, cleared after Save).
- **Splits** ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-4, OQ-6): the discount sheet is on the bill only and is hidden once a split is closed; closed splits show as locked with their invoice; lines added later appear in the open remainder with a "new" mark; a void / comp on a locked line shows `LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT` with "Reopen the bill to change a paid split".

---

## §9. Error catalogue additions (Foundation §6.2; appended to the status map and `@pos/domain/src/errors.ts` at build, no bump on its own, R34)

| Code | Status | `details` | Returned when |
|---|---|---|---|
| `BILL_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | `{ bill_id }` | unknown bill id |
| `SHIFT_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | `{ shift_id }` | unknown shift |
| `INVOICE_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | `{ invoice_id }` | reprint of an unknown invoice |
| `PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | `{ payment_id }` | remove of an unknown / already removed payment |
| `PAYMENT_MODE_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | `{ mode_id }` | mode unknown or inactive in the branch settings |
| `PETTY_CASH_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | `{ entry_id }` | settle of an unknown advance |
| `Z_REPORT_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | `{ business_date }` | reprint of a Z that does not exist |
| `BILL_NOT_OPEN` | 409 | `{ bill_id, state }` | the bill / split is closed, absorbed, or its order cancelled |
| `BILL_CLOSE_BLOCKED` | 409 | `{ reason: 'void_pending' \| 'weight_pending' \| 'tax_unresolved', order_item_ids }` (≤ 40) ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6: `lines_unassigned` dropped), ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2: `tax_unresolved`) | §3.3 rules 2, 3, 3a |
| `DISCOUNT_PENDING` | 409 | `{ alert_id }` | a discount request on the bill is open (close, second discount) |
| `BUYER_ID_REQUIRED` | 409 | `{ threshold_minor, net_minor, accepted_types: string[] }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-4)) | §3.3 rule 6 |
| `BILL_TOTAL_CHANGED` | 409 | `{ expected_minor, total_minor }` | §3.3 rule 7 |
| `BILL_BALANCE_DUE` | 409 | `{ split_no, balance_minor }` | §3.3 rule 8 |
| `BILL_PARTLY_PAID` | 409 | `{ bill_id }` | `ApplyDiscount` / `RemoveDiscount` on a bill with a closed split (B7) ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-4, OQ-6: was "any line edit on a partly paid bill") |
| `LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT` ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6) | 409 | `{ order_item_id, bill_id, split_no }` | a remove / comp / comp removal / weight / void touching units a closed split invoiced (B15, rows Q-3, V-1) |
| `BILL_HAS_PAYMENTS` | 409 | `{ bill_id, split_nos? }` | merge / seating split with payments; a re-plan that drops a paid split |

| `SHIFT_NOT_OPEN` | 409 | `{ reason: 'no_shift' \| 'no_drawer_shift' \| 'not_open' \| 'payment_shift_closed' }` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2): `shift_counted` dropped, no counted state) (⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-26)) | money with no money shift; drawer action off a drawer; count of a closed shift |

G0-7's `REFUND_EXCEEDS_PAID` is not created (no refund command, Q8).

## §10. Tests (`apps/hub/test/billing/*.int.test.ts`, `packages/domain/src/billing/*.test.ts`, `apps/hub/test/billing/fiscal/*.test.ts`; client tests in `apps/client/src/features/billing/`)

Every command also has: happy path, one test per error code it returns, retransmit returns the identical response and writes nothing (D32), and every grant state of its permission (`allowed`, `requires_approval`, not granted, out of scope; 04 G1-7 pattern). **HIGH** tests (G0 §5, G1-13) are marked **H**.

### 10.1 Bill, splits, discount, charges

1. Projected bill (no 05 event yet) equals the bill after `BillOpened` for the same lines (same `computeBill`).
2. Dine-in bill follows `seating_orders` (only orders neither billed nor cancelled, ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 H1-21; a closed seating's billed orders never appear as a projected bill): a second round joins; a cancelled order leaves; an archived table's order stays billable (01a §6.5).
3. Takeaway bill = one `bill_key` (the root order and its additions, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-25)); close allowed before any `ItemServed` (Q2, B16); afterwards `RemoveItems` / `VoidItems` → `ORDER_BILLED`.
4. Lines: COMP shown at value with deduction; PENDING VOID tagged and only `refund_minor` subtracted (a comped unit's `comp_voided_minor` not); return line base 0; pending weight 0 and listed in `blocks`.
5b. ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-11): a fully comped (zero-total) bill closes with no payment via `bill_ref`.
5. **H** close refused for each §3.3 rule 1–9, in order, each with its code; nothing written (no event, outbox, job, buyer row).
6. `BillClosed` reaches the staff sockets and cashier kiosks (05 registers it in `TABLE_EVENT_TYPES`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-7)); `OrdersUnbilled` reaches the map / table view; the tile turns NEEDS_CLEANING; takeaway writes no `BillClosed`.
7. Item split: units over 2 splits; a deal whole; an option child with its parent; `units_missing`, `units_exceed`, `deal_whole`, `option_with_parent` refusals ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6).
8. Amount split: Σ shares must equal the default-class total (`shares_sum`); equal into 3 of Rs 1,000.00 → 333.33 / 333.33 / 333.34 net shares by `allocateDealMinor` (remainder to the last split, OQ-5) ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-13); a later round re-bases the last open share (H1-22).
9. Re-plan keeps a paid split's number; dropping it → `BILL_HAS_PAYMENTS`; after a close only the open remainder re-plans, naming a closed split → `BILL_SPLIT_INVALID { reason: 'split_closed' }` ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6).
10. **H** property: for random bills (1–40 lines, 1–3 classes, discounts, service charge, item / amount / equal splits, random payment classes) Σ split `S` = Σ line bases; Σ printed tax = Σ invoice tax rows; one rounding per split; no negative figure.
10b. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6) property over random bills with rounds added between split closes: Σ closed-split units + open-remainder units = the bill's live line units; no unit in two splits; every closed split's invoiced units and totals unchanged by later rounds, voids and comps on other lines.
10c. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6): after split 1 closes, a new round joins the highest-numbered open split with no staff action and no close block; when the last split closes the bill is CLOSED; a later order on that seating (where 02a allows one) opens a **new** bill with its own `BillOpened`.
10f. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-4): 3 × Rs 1,000, comp 1 before an item-split close (split 1 = 1 unit, share 666), then `VoidItems` 2 units → `LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT` (residual base 334 on 0 open units). ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-2): line B 3 × Rs 1,000, 1 unit closed in split 1: comp 1 open unit → open part = 2,000 − 1,000 = 1,000 (not 1,333); comp 3 units → `LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT`; a weight change on a line with closed units → refused; Σ closed shares + open base = line base after every step.
10d. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-4): property over random bases (incl. the review's `[99900, 99900, 99900, 100]` with 299 700, and run 2's base 10 000 / 99.99 % / units 2-2-1 per-part case, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-12)) — `allocateCappedMinor`: Σ shares = total, `0 ≤ share ≤ base`, deterministic tie order; no split net, tax or total below 0.
10e. ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-6): item split `{B: 1}` closed in split 1, `{B: 2}` open in split 2; a 1-unit void of B → accepted, taken from split 2 (plan `{B: 1}`); a 2-unit void → `LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT`; Σ closed + open = live units after each.
11. **H** tax table tests: exclusive and inclusive prices; 500 / 1600 bp; half-up at each named point only; a BigInt-sized product (Rs 9,999,999 × 1600 bp) exact. ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2, J-3): **Pakistan regression fixture** — every v0.6.2 compute fixture (incl. §3.6's worked examples and §6.31) run through the v0.7 `computeSplit` with the `PK-PB` and `none` seeds gives byte-identical totals, tax rows and rounding.
11b. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2) stacked taxes from data only (a test-only jurisdiction fixture shaped like a federal + provincial pair, e.g. 500 bp simple + 800 bp simple, and a 500 bp + 950 bp **compound** pair): two tax rows per profile in list order; each rounded half up at its own point; the compound row's base includes the first row's rounded tax; Σ rows = invoice tax; a profile with one tax never shows a second row; no command or query takes a tax choice (schema check: no tax field in any 05 command payload).
11c. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-3) property, **both price modes**: for random bills (1–40 lines, 1–3 profiles of 1–3 taxes, simple / compound, discounts, service charge, item / amount / equal splits, both classes) — `G = N + SC + Σ tax rows`; no negative figure; inclusive with no discount, no SC and one class → `G` = Σ menu amounts exactly; the inclusive back-calculation rounds once per profile and `apportionMinor` spreads it with Σ = `TI_p`; the discount is always taken before tax; SC is computed on the pre-tax net in both modes (OQ-10, final in v0.7.1). ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-12): `apportionMinor` by exact shares — the review's case gives [125, 27].
12. Deal taxed under the deal's class on its whole billed amount; option child under its own; never from `allocated_price_minor` (README :214).
13. **H** G1-15 (restated in v0.7, J-2): a bill with lines in two profiles shows each profile's tax rows on its own lines' base only; no line in two profiles; `tax_enabled = false` or a null profile → no tax; on the `PK-PB` seed every profile has exactly one tax row (no Mutfak stacking, M-4).
13b. ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2): a taxed line whose profile has no tax list in the branch's jurisdiction, or no rate row in force → bill view `blocks` `tax_unresolved`, close refused `BILL_CLOSE_BLOCKED { reason: 'tax_unresolved' }`; under `none` every profile resolves from `rate_bp`.
14. **H** rate by date: a rate row effective tomorrow is not used today; at the effective date it is; the bill view shows the row's ref; an owner override (per tax class and tax code) beats the catalog row; jurisdiction `none` uses `rate_bp`; a credit copies its original's rates even after a rate change (B5). ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (Q-1 provisional): a bill reopened with no line change after a rate change (or `PK` → `PK-PB`) → the credit copies the original's tax and snapshot, the new invoice uses today's rates, the difference is paid in or out.
14b. ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1): jurisdiction data — a `jurisdiction_rules` row effective tomorrow is not used today; the branch overrides (price mode, rounding step / mode / `applies_to`, ⚠️ v0.7.2 H9-3) win over the jurisdiction's; switching `PK` → `PK-PB` (⚠️ v0.7.2 H9-3) sets `switched_at`, later invoices record `PK-PB` / `PRA`, earlier ones keep `PK`; every invoice records `jurisdiction_id`, `adapter_key`, `currency_code`, `prices_include_tax`. ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-9): an open bill keeps its price mode across a mode change and a reopen (credit and new invoice equal with no line change). ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-2): an open or reopened bill keeps its rounding rule across a rounding-setting change (credit and new invoice equal with no line change).
14c. ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-1, row F-6): `currencyExponent('PKR') = 2`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-3): `none` gives **no rounding** (step 1 minor unit) for an exponent-0, -2 and -3 currency (test-only fixtures), `PK` / `PK-PB` round to 100; and an exponent-0 / -3 currency gives defaults `variance_threshold_minor` / `petty_cash_limit_minor` of 100 / 5 000 major units; a jurisdiction whose currency differs from the **tenant's** (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2, owner Q-1) → `422 CONFIG_REFERENCE_INVALID { reason: 'currency_mismatch' }` (01a A-1 test). ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.3 (H10-4, H10-7): with no `billing` key the hub's built-in rules equal the `PK` seed (PKR tenant) or the `none` seed (else), asserted against the seed rows; `seed-tenant --currency PKX` is refused, nothing written.
15b. ~~`pro_rata` reopen case~~ ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26): removed with `pro_rata`.
15. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26) (replaces the `pro_rata` worked examples): `higher_rate` mixed split on `PK-PB` — card 3,000 + cash 3,624 on Rs 5,710 net → the whole split at 16 % → 6,624.00, any payment order; all card → 5 % → 5,996.00; a cash refund on a card bill refused (type paid nothing); a catalog row with `pro_rata` is refused by `0016`'s CHECK (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-10)). ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-1): unpaid Rs 5,710 → `card_due` 5,996.00 and a card of exactly 5,996.00 is accepted and closes; `cash_due` 6,624.00. ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.3 (H17-1): card 5,996.00 paid → `cash_due` 0 and a cash `in` → `PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_BALANCE { balance_minor: 0 }`; reopen −1.00 base → `cash_due` 0, card refund 2.00.
15e. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-2), ⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25), ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26) property: for random bills, payment sets and every rounding rule (`all`, `cash`, `none`), **every permutation of payment order and every choice of shifts closing between payments gives the same tax rows, the same `R` and the same total** for the same set of payment classes; tax rows never depend on `R`; a reopen with no line change gives the credited total and tax.
16b. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-5, owner R3): 10 % on Rs 2,000 (A in split 1, B in split 2); split 1 closes (D 100); void B → split 2 total 0, open discount 0 (never negative), split 1 unchanged; void half of B instead → open discount = min(100, B's remaining base) re-spread capped; no invoice below 0.
16c. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-8, owner Q-1): 10 % on Rs 2,000 (A split 1, B split 2); split 1 closes (D 100); a Rs 1,000 round C joins split 2; void B → open discount = min(100, B's remaining base) = 0, C pays full Rs 1,000; reopen clears `bill_discount_lines`.
16. Discount amount / percent on the **whole bill**, spread over every line by `allocateCappedMinor` (⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-16)) before splitting, each split carrying its lines' shares; tax after discount (PRA example); `DISCOUNT_TOO_LARGE`; after a split closed `ApplyDiscount` / `RemoveDiscount` → `BILL_PARTLY_PAID` and the frozen amount stays; lines joining later carry no discount ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-4).
17. Discount `requires_approval` → request; approve → `DiscountApplied { alert_id, approver_staff_id = answerer }`; close refused while pending (`DISCOUNT_PENDING`); withdraw → nothing; the bill closed meanwhile → `apply` throws, the answer is refused.
18. **H** sticky charge (M-3; ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-9): "Ask for bill" after a removal prints no charge and sets "check bill printed"): remove → reopen / check-bill print / split / merge / move / bill view / re-close → still removed; `ApplyServiceCharge` re-adds with `source: 'manual'`.
19. Service charge rate is the order type's at bill creation; a settings change later does not change an open bill.
20. Rounding step 100 / 500 / 1 000, methods nearest / down / up; the rounding line on the receipt.
20b. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25) named cases (rounding adjustment model; v0.7.3–v0.7.8 cases superseded, kept in the history files). Pakistan (`PK-PB`, `higher_rate`, `all` Rs 1): card-only 5 995.50 → total 5 996.00, `R` +0.50 untaxed, tax row 285.50 unchanged, card 5 996.00 closes; cash 6 623.60 → 6 624.00. (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26): the `pro_rata` cases are removed.) Refunds ("jitna liya utna wapas"): **full refund** — `higher_rate` mixed bill paid card 3 000.00 + cash 3 624.00 (total 6 624.00 incl. rounding +0.40), every line removed → card out 3 000.00 + cash out 3 624.00, exactly what was taken, rounding included → `BillClosedEmpty`; partial — card bill 2 100.00, reopen −200.00 base → new exact 1 890.40, re-rounded 1 890.00 → card out 210.00, a cash out refused (cash paid nothing); mixed card 10.00 + cash 2.00 on 12.02 (`cash` step 5) → reopen to 7.01 → total 7.00 (cash involved) → refund 5.00, card ≤ 10.00, cash ≤ 2.00, both shown, cash a step multiple; no-change reopen → refund 0, same tax and total. `applies_to: 'cash'` (step 5): card-only 12.02 → `R` 0, total 12.02; card 10.00 + cash 2.00 → `R` −0.02, total 12.00; a cash amount off the step → `PAYMENT_CASH_STEP`; ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26) (H15-5): exponent 0, step 5 `up`, cash total 12 050 (exact 12 049): card 33 first → shown cash due **12 015** (rounded down), cash 12 015 + card 2 closes; a cash refund due off the step is shown rounded down. ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-4, Q-2): `cash` step 5 — the check bill of an unpaid 13.99 split prints 14.00; a 0.02 split closes at 0 with no payment (rounding −0.02). ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.3 (H17-1): Rs 5,710 on `PK-PB` paid 5,996.00 by card → no cash due (before close, and on a no-change reopen); reopen −1.00 base owing Rs 2 → only "refund card 2.00", no cash due.
20c. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25) **property** (the run-12 – run-14 model, rewritten for the rounding-adjustment model): ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.1 (§26): three setups — `higher_rate` × {`all` Rs 1, `cash` step 5, `none`} (`pro_rata` out of v1.0) — with class rates equal and different (Punjab 500 / 1600 bp); 30 000 random bills each, two seeds: (1) **sales** — random partial card / cash payments in random order, each within the pad's accepted range → every split reaches `B = 0` and closes; tax rows identical to the same bill with `R` forced to 0; `R` never in a tax row; every shown due is accepted as keyed and closes as the class it implies (H15-5, H15-6, H16-1); the rounding rule is the bill's frozen one (H16-2); (2) **refunds** — reopen with random line removals and additions (incl. under half a step and new rounds), then pay the shown refund / payment dues per type in random order → every split reaches `B = 0`; no `out` exceeds what its type paid; payment class and tax never change by a refund; **full refunds** return each type exactly what it paid; (3) **no-change reopens** — every closed bill reopened with no line change → 0 stuck, 0 changes of `G`, `R`, tax or total. Zero stuck splits is the pass condition; the generator is seeded and prints its seed on failure. ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2: the no-change-reopen sweep excludes reopens across a rate or jurisdiction change (Q-1), which #14 covers. ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.3 (H17-1): while `B ≤ 0` both `in` dues are 0 and no `in` is accepted (every setup, sale and refund phases).

### 10.2 Payments, close, invoices

21. ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-2, H1-3): cash `amount` 430 000 tendered 500 000 → change 70 000 on **that payment's** shift; expected cash +430 000; two cash payments on two shifts each keep their own change. ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-3), ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.3 (H17-3): a cash amount above `cash_due` → `PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_BALANCE` (a cash `in` above the balance but ≤ `cash_due` is accepted).
22. Card above balance → `PAYMENT_EXCEEDS_BALANCE`; reference required → `PAYMENT_REFERENCE_REQUIRED`.
23. **H** property ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-2): Σ in `amount` − Σ out `amount` = split total at every close; change = Σ (tendered − amount) ≤ Σ cash tendered; Σ shifts' expected cash moves = Σ cash `amount` in − out.
24. Mode switch recomputes the total in the `AddPayment` result (M-7).
25b. ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-9, owner R4): reopen a day-1 bill on day 2 → `RemovePayment` of the day-1 payment → `SHIFT_NOT_OPEN { reason: 'payment_shift_closed' }`; the lower new total is settled by an `out` payment on today's shift; shift A's expected and count unchanged.
25. `RemovePayment` own; another's needs `close-shift-for-others` (`not_own_payment`).
26. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-1): sale and credit `invoice_ref`s never collide (`AC1-S000001` vs `AC1-C000001`); gap-free numbers: 200 closes interleaved with rollbacks (forced failures) leave numbers 1..200 with no gap; credit series separate.
27. `expected_total_minor` stale after a void approval between view and close → `BILL_TOTAL_CHANGED`.
28. **H** race: two kiosks close the same split → one invoice, the other `BILL_NOT_OPEN`; a void approved while closing → close refused or void refused (`ORDER_BILLED`), never both through.
29. Last split writes `OrdersBilled` (all the bill's orders) and `BillClosed` once; earlier splits write neither.
30. Each split close closes open `comp` requests on the units it invoiced `obsolete`; the last close the rest (02b To 05) ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-23).
31. Pending void blocks close (04 `pendingVoids`); after approve the bill drops the line and closes.
32. Partly paid bill ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-6): `RemoveItems`, `CompLine`, `SetLineWeight`, `RemoveComp`, `VoidItems` on units a closed split invoiced → `LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT` (rows Q-3, V-1); the same on a line only in the open remainder → accepted; after a reopen → accepted.
33. Pending weight with `live_units > 0` blocks; a voided pending-weight line (`live_units = 0`) does not (04 R-4).
34. Receipt `Doc`: number, lines, discount, service charge, one tax row per (tax label, rate, payment class) (⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-2)), rounding, total, payments, change, every seller tax registration, one line each with its label (⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-5): two registrations → two lines) / header / footer per template, "FISCAL NUMBER PENDING" when fiscalized and not answered, drawer block only with cash. ⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25): the "Rounding adjustment" line is outside every tax row and no tax row changes with it; a card-only bill rounds too (Pakistan).
35. No printer routed → close succeeds, `receipt.job_id = null`, logged with ids.
36. **H** forced COMMIT failure on `CloseSplit` → no event, no outbox row, no print job, no `invoice_buyers` row, no `processed_commands` row.

### 10.3 Reopen, remove comp, buyer ID

37. **H** reopen a closed 2-split bill → two `InvoiceCredited` (credit series 1, 2, each the full original), `OrdersUnbilled`, splits open with payments; re-close → new sale numbers; dine-in full edit writes `BillClosed` again (#37b), corrections writes none ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-25).
37b. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-10 + S-9, owner R2) dine-in full-edit reopen (seating NEEDS_CLEANING, no other bill): `SeatingReopened` (reaches the map and table-view sockets, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-7); a PARTLY_PAID reopen writes none, H2-27), table ORDERED / FOOD_ON_TABLE, holder's `SubmitOrder` and `RemoveItems` pass the lock, re-close writes `BillClosed` → NEEDS_CLEANING again.

### 10.4 Shifts, petty cash, drawer

45. Open shift with float; second → `SHIFT_ALREADY_OPEN`; drawer taken → `DRAWER_IN_USE`.
46. **H** ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT): `CountShift` closes the shift and frees the drawer in one transaction; the next `OpenShift { as_drawer }` on that kiosk succeeds at once. Blind count: every `shift` query, `CloseShift` and X response before `ShiftCounted` has `expected_minor = null` and no cash sum; the `CountShift` response is the first with it (a `HandoverCount` reveals only its own handover's expected, §6.19).
47. **H** property (expected cash, G1-8, Q10): random payments (two takers on one drawer), change, outs, advances, settles (on another shift), cash-outs, cash-ins → the fold's expected = §3.7 formula; Σ shifts' takings = Z net + carried forward − brought forward, by mode (⚠️ v0.7.2 H9-12; §6.26).
48. Takeover on a drawer kiosk: the second person's cash and card land in the drawer shift with `taken_by` = them; the drawer close shows the breakdown by taker.
49. Order taker with `take-payment` (`own_tables`) and own shift: collects on own table only (`outside_scope` otherwise) into own shift.

50c. ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-1): a day-1 variance answered on day 2 → day-2 Z "variance sign-offs" line (shift date day 1, outcome, approver); still pending at day 2's close → day-2 Z "pending sign-offs"; X shows both live; takings unchanged.
50b. ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT): a variance above the threshold → shift CLOSED, `variance_state = 'pending'`, sign-off request; the day close is **not** blocked and the Z shows "pending sign-off"; a later approve / reject on the next day → `approved` / `disputed`; no payment ever lands on that shift.
50. ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (Q-2, owner 2026-11-18 11:08–11:09 PKT): variance within threshold → closed, no request; above → closed + sign-off; a Manager approves (holders right, H1-1) → `ShiftVarianceSignedOff { outcome: 'approved' }`; reject → `outcome: 'disputed'`, no recount, no reopen.

53. ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-6): `CountShift` while a petty-cash request waits → the request is closed `obsolete` (`shift_closed`) and listed in the result. ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-2, H6-15, Q-2): an over-limit petty cash still open at the count is closed `obsolete` then (H7-6), ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-9); no refusal reveals expected cash. Petty cash (over-limit request answered by a Manager through the holders right, H1-1): advance + settle (spent + returned = amount, else `PETTY_SETTLE_MISMATCH`), out, in; over the limit → request; `requires_approval` grant → request always.
54. Open advances listed at shift close and on X / Z; never block.

### 10.5 Merges, splits of tables, printing

56. Merge with no payments: the absorbed bill folds into the survivor; a removed service charge on the survivor stays removed.
57. Merge / seating split with payments → `BILL_HAS_PAYMENTS` (05 guard after 04's), not `MERGE_BLOCKED_BY_VOID` ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-7); a seating split of a **reopened** bill with no payment left → `BILL_HAS_PAYMENTS` too (⚠️ Changed in v0.5.1 (H4-3)).
58. `BillRequested` → check bill at the zone's bill printer → floor → default; no 01d notice raised; a replayed queued `RequestBill` prints once.
59. Check bill `Doc`: banner, no invoice number / fiscal number / QR, default-class total, digital-rate line; `CheckBillPrinted` audit row with ids and counts.
60. **Print to:** a waiting / failed job → `RetargetBillPrint` moves it; a printed one → `PRINT_JOB_NOT_PENDING`; nothing prints twice.
61. Reprint after `FiscalNumberAssigned` shows the fiscal number and the QR block; before it, "FISCAL NUMBER PENDING".

### 10.6 Day close, reports

62. Day close refused: void pending (04 first), then open shift, then unbilled orders — each with its list (≤ 40) and `count`.
63b. ⚠️ Changed in v0.5 (H3-3) + a PARTLY_PAID carry: day 1 split 1 paid 500 and closed, split 2 paid 200, bill carried → takings 700 = Z 500 + CF 200; day 2 split 2 paid 300 and closed → 300 = Z 500 + 0 − BF 200. ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-9, owner R4), ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-3): carry-over of a bill with Rs 3,000 paid → close allowed; day-1 Z: carried forward 3,000; day-2 Z: the invoice, brought forward 3,000; a day-1 bill reopened and re-closed on day 2 at the same total → Z2 net 0, takings 0; reopened on day 2 and left open → Z2 net −1,000, carried forward 1,000; the §6.26 invariant holds every day.

63c. ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-10): carry the same order on day 1 and day 2 → two `carried_orders` rows, no key error, rebuild equal.

64. Appender closes open 05 requests and 02b comp requests `obsolete` (`day_closed`); void requests never reach it.

66. **H** Z invariant (restated, H1-9 / owner R4, ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 H2-3: Σ takings = Z net + carried forward − brought forward) over a generated day (sales, credits, reopens, splits, mixed classes, petty cash, three shifts): Z net = Σ sale − Σ credit; tax by (tax code, profile, class) = Σ invoice tax rows; voids by disposition from `ItemVoided`; replay from seq 0 gives the identical Z. ⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-5): no staff name in `ZReportIssued.payload`.
67. X = the same builder over the open day; uncounted shifts' expected hidden.

### 10.7 Fiscal

69. **H** outbox row written in the close transaction (fold); a rolled-back close has none; `not_required` for adapter `none`, a non-fiscalized order type (delivery default off, Q17) and invoices before `switched_at`.
70. **H** crash between the HTTP answer and the `FiscalNumberAssigned` commit (fault injection) → restart resends the **same idempotency key** (`invoice_ref`); one `FiscalNumberAssigned` in the log.
71. **H** order ⚠️ Changed in v0.4 (H2-18), ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-6): with an adapter whose `ordering` is `strict_per_pos_id`, a backing-off head row of `pos_id` 1 holds only `pos_id` 1's later rows; `pos_id` 2 with a missing credential never holds `pos_id` 1; with a fake adapter whose `ordering` is `none`, a backing-off row holds nothing; in both a credit waits for its original's answer and steps aside.
72. Back-off schedule with a fake clock; `RetryFiscalNow` pulls `next_try_at`; a `rejected` row is not resent.
73. Stuck alert (⚠️ Changed in v0.3, H1-18: expires at day close, re-raised on the next worker pass while stuck; H1-17: a credit of a rejected original is rejected): pending older than `stuck_after_ms`, a rejected row, or half the catch-up window → one `fiscal_outbox_stuck` to managers; closed when clear; details counts only.
74b. ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-4), ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-4), ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.2 (H8-3), ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-5): a payment taken while the branch was on jurisdiction `none` on a bill closed after the switch, in a mode with no code for the new adapter → close refused `PAYMENT_MODE_NOT_FISCAL { mode_id, adapter_key }`; a non-fiscalized order type (delivery) in such a mode → accepted, `not_required`; `AddPayment` in a mode with codes only for another adapter key → refused; on `none` → accepted; the submission's `payments[].authority_code` = the mode's code for the branch's adapter; an adapter that still receives no code → `rejected` (`mode_unmapped`), not a wait. The PRA mapping cases (codes 1 / 2 / 5, item tax = total tax, service-charge item, credit's buyer and `pos_id`) are Appendix P test P-T2.
74. ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-6): port contract with a **fake adapter** (test-only): `submitSale` / `submitCredit` receive the neutral submission of §3.15 built from `InvoiceIssued` / `InvoiceCredited` + `invoice_buyers` + `fiscal_credentials` (lines with per-tax rows, payments with `authority_code`, buyer `{ name, tax_id }` or null, idempotency key = `invoice_ref`, a credit's `original` with the original's fiscal number); each answer outcome (`ok`, `retry`, `rejected`, `duplicate` with and without a number) gives §3.15's row transition; the core never branches on the adapter key (one test suite runs against the fake and the PRA adapter). The PRA adapter's own fixtures are Appendix P test P-T1. ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-4): the submission also carries `seller.tax_registrations`, `config { environment, base_url, settings }` (the branch's blob for that adapter), line `item_id` / `tax_profile_id` / `fiscal_category`, line-tax `payment_class` / `base_minor` and `original.issued_at`; an invalid settings blob → `422` at the `PUT` and `adapter_settings_invalid` on the hub. ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.3 (H10-5, H10-6): `credentials` carries every `credentialNames` entry from `fiscal_credentials`; a pending row of an earlier adapter after a jurisdiction switch is sent with `base_urls[row.adapter_key]` and `adapter_settings[row.adapter_key]`; a `settingsSchema` with a field marked secret fails a registration test.

77. `fiscal-status` and every log line of the worker contain no token, no buyer data (captured logger).

**Close-out.** 44 E2E flows (webapp-testing: dine-in split + mixed payment; takeaway before food; reopen; day close with carry-over); 45 `test:perf` row (200-bill rush close on the pilot box, never in the gate); 46 manual checks (owner): PRA sandbox run with the owner's sandbox token (G1-12), receipt QR scan on each printer model (A3), drawer kick on the kiosk printer, go-live checklist (G1-16).

Estimate: 18 phase-0 + 34 hub + 10 client + 3 close-out = **65 tasks** (above the ~60 budget; accepted by OQ-2 (b), README R38). **Plan phases** ⚠️ Changed in v0.2 (OQ-2): **05-1** bills, payments, close, reopen, fiscal (hub tasks 1–20, 24, 27–31a and their client tasks); **05-2** shifts, petty cash, drawer, day close, X / Z (21–23, 25, 26, 32, 33). Phase 05-1 needs only `OpenShift` and the shift fold from 05-2, which it builds first (tasks 8, 15). ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-14): `apps/hub/src/billing/index.ts` is created in task 7 and **each task registers what it adds**; the audit maps of 05-1's events (task 32's part) ship in 05-1 together with 0023's `audit` checkpoint delete; 05-2 adds only its own maps (its events have no history before it, so no second refold).

## §12. Definition of Done, checklist, questions, deferred

### 12.1 Definition of Done

1. Every §4.5 row applied with its marker, version and test; README R37 and row 05 updated.
2. Every §10 test exists with its number in its name and passes in the normal gate (`TMPDIR=/dev/shm …`); HIGH tasks reviewed in their own PR.
3. Every command: happy path, one test per code, retransmit, every grant state; every query: fixed statement count at 1 and 40.
4. No buyer ID, token, PIN, name or phone in any log line (captured-logger tests #43, #77).
5. Rebuild of `billing`, `shifts`, `audit` from seq 0 equals live.
6. Owner manual checks (§11 #46) done on the pilot hardware before go-live; go-live preconditions met or the branch on `PK` (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-9)).

### 12.3 Open questions

**Owner questions (product choices)** — ⚠️ Changed in v0.2: **all answered** (owner, 2026-11-17 23:11–23:15 PKT; `docs/stories/05-g1-decisions.md` §9). The options and Rec are kept as asked; the answer ends each row.

| # | Question | Options | Rec |
|---|---|---|---|
| OQ-1 | **What does a bill reopen do to 02b's BILLED** (G1-3 design point)? | (a) **02b bump `OrdersUnbilled` (row Q-2): the orders are unbilled again, so line fixes go through 02b / 04 as usual (void with approval, comp removal), and the next close writes `OrdersBilled` again**; (b) the orders stay BILLED; a reopened bill allows only bill-level edits (discount, charges, payments) and "credit lines" kept by 05 outside 02b's ledger; (c) the orders stay BILLED; a reopen is only a credit + one fresh 05-built invoice re-entered by hand | **(a)** — one ledger (G0-2), voids keep their approval and wastage record; (b) makes a second line ledger in 05; (c) re-keys food — ⚠️ Changed in v0.2: **Answered (a)** (owner 2026-11-17 23:11–23:15 PKT): reopen writes 02b `OrdersUnbilled` (row Q-2); the orders return to unbilled, 02b adds and changes lines normally, the day-close guard sees them as unbilled. |
| OQ-2 | **Spec size:** 29 hub endpoints + 3 cloud routes = 32, ~64 build tasks (budget ~30 / ~60). Split? | (a) split at v0.2 into **05a Bills + payments + fiscal** and **05b Shifts + petty cash + day close + Z**; (b) **keep one spec, record a README decision (R38) as for 00's cloud part, and build in two plan phases (05-1 bills / fiscal, 05-2 shifts / day close)** | **(b)** — every payment needs a money shift (B11) and the Z needs both halves, so a split spec has a two-way dependency (05a needs 05b's shift, 05b's Z needs 05a's invoices); two plan phases give the same review size — ⚠️ Changed in v0.2: **Answered (b)**: one spec, two plan phases (05-1 / 05-2, §11); the >30 endpoint budget exception is README **R38** with this reason. |
| OQ-3 | Where is the PRA token entered? | (a) **hub CLI `set-fiscal-token` into the hub config file (row H-2), by the installer / developer at go-live**; (b) a write-only field on the hub admin page | **(a)** — no HTTP path ever carries the token; (b) later if owners must rotate tokens themselves — ⚠️ Changed in v0.2: **Answered (b)**: hub admin page field (§6.28a, B22, row H-2): `manage-fiscal-settings` (Owner only by default, row B-1), write-only ("set on <date>" only), replace = enter a new one, hub-only (never synced, logged or in events), audit `fiscal_token_changed` with ids only. v0.1's CLI is dropped. |
| OQ-4 | Discount target: G1 Q6 text says "on the bill only"; G0 Q6 (a), which the owner chose, says "on the bill or a split" | (a) **bill or one open split** (G0 (a) as chosen); (b) bill only | **(a)** — a split payer asking for a discount is the same customer case; spec written as (a) — ⚠️ Changed in v0.2: **Answered (b)**: discount on the **whole bill only**; refused with `409 BILL_PARTLY_PAID` once any split closed; spread pro rata over the lines before splitting (B7, §3.5, §6.6). |
| OQ-5 | Amount / equal splits when the adapter is PRA: each split is its own invoice with every line at its share (A8 open) | (a) **as written: proportional lines per split invoice**; (b) with a fiscal adapter, amount / equal splits become several payments on **one** invoice (one fiscal record), item splits stay separate invoices | **(a)** until PRA answers A8; (b) is a setting change if PRA refuses fractional lines — ⚠️ Changed in v0.2: **Answered (a)**: one invoice per amount / equal split with lines allocated proportionally (`allocateDealMinor`, remainder to the last); still subject to A8. |
| OQ-6 | A partly paid bill (one split closed): may lines still change? | (a) **frozen: line edits refused until the bill closes; a fix is a reopen** (B15); (b) lines outside closed item splits stay editable | **(a)** — amount splits have no line ownership; one rule is safer — ⚠️ Changed in v0.2: **Answered (owner rule, (b) made simple)**: a closed split is frozen; the unpaid remainder stays open; a new line after a split closed joins the open remainder automatically; all splits closed = bill closed, a new item on that seating opens a new bill; changing a closed split = reopen (B15, §3.8, `LINE_IN_CLOSED_SPLIT`, rows Q-3 / V-1, tests 10b, 10c, 32). |
| OQ-7 | Who approves over-limit petty cash and shift variances (the requester's grant is `allowed`)? | (a) **roles holding `close-shift-for-others` allowed at branch (row B-3)**; (b) a billing setting listing approver roles | **(a)** — no new setting; the same people close others' shifts — ⚠️ Changed in v0.2: **Answered (a)**: holders of `close-shift-for-others` (Manager, Owner by default); no separate setting. |
| OQ-8 | How long does the hub keep buyer IDs (`invoice_buyers`)? | (a) **as long as the invoice (legal record); never synced**; (b) delete after the fiscal ack + N days (reprints then show no buyer) | **(a)** until the accountant says otherwise — ⚠️ Changed in v0.2: **Answered (a)**: buyer ID kept as long as the invoice, hub-only, never synced. |

### 12.5 Hostile self-review (v0.1)

- **Races:** close vs void approve, two kiosks on one split, discount approved while closing, reopen approved twice, day close vs a close in flight — all in one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` with the checks inside (tests 28, 17, 39, 62). An approval `apply` re-checks state read-only and throws when the bill moved on (01d refuses the answer).
- **Idempotency:** every command by `command_id`; the outbox by `invoice_id`; the PRA resend by USIN (A10 open). A queued `RequestBill` replay prints the check bill again — accepted (a reprint, no money).
- **N+1:** bill view ≤ 9, open bills 3, close ≤ 18, X ≤ 8, day close +≤ 10 — fixed counts (test 91).
- **Indexes:** each query in §5 names its index; the line guard uses `bill_orders_order`; the worker `fiscal_outbox_pending`.
- **Privacy:** buyer ID in one non-synced table, full ID on that customer's receipt only (owner Q-1), captured-logger tests; tokens only in the hub `fiscal_credentials` table, write-only (OQ-3); names never in logs; audit details ids and counts.
- **External failure:** internet down → close works, outbox waits, stuck alert after 30 min; PRA 4xx → rejected + alert, never silently resent; printer down → close works, Print to.
- **Contradictions checked:** status codes §6 ↔ §9; permissions ↔ row B-1; F §4.4 BILLED ↔ row F-2; 03a's `stockEffects` order ↔ row A-4; 04's guards stay first.
- **Over-engineering cut:** no separate payments service, no cache, no batch fiscal, no refund command, no per-split discount reasons, no snapshot V5; `bill_print_jobs` kept only because "Print to" needs the job id (G1-10); `shift_takings` kept only for the per-taker breakdown at a fixed statement count (Q10).
- **Known risk:** the PRA field mapping beyond the research is taken from PRA's spec v1.2 at build (task 30); an unclear field stops the build for the owner, it is not guessed.

### 12.6 Hostile self-review (v0.7) ⚠️ Changed in v0.7

- **Pakistan unchanged:** every rule that moved to data has its v0.6.2 value in the `none` / `PK-PB` seed (A-1); the one-tax formulas of §3.5 reduce to v0.6.2's exactly (inclusive back-calculation, half-up points, SC, rounding); test #11 runs every v0.6.2 fixture through v0.7.
- **Money tables:** `invoices` (jurisdiction, adapter, currency, price mode), `invoice_taxes` (profile × tax code × class with seq / compound / label), `invoice_buyers` (type + value), `fiscal_outbox` (adapter key) and 0021's `tax_profile_id` take their final shape before the first merge; no second migration is needed for another jurisdiction.
- **Privacy:** buyer tax IDs of every type follow one rule set (B19); `hashOmit` still drops the whole `buyer`; `VALIDATION_FAILED` paths name `tax_id.value` / `.type`, never the value; the submission carries the value only to the adapter.
- **Contradictions checked:** B4 / §3.4 / §3.5 / §6.12 / §5 agree on per-tax rows; `PAYMENT_MODE_NOT_FISCAL` details ↔ §6.9 / §6.12 / §9; `BILL_REOPEN_NOT_SUPPORTED` ↔ §3.15 / §6.13 / §9 / §12.2; A-1 field renames (`tax_registration_no`, `service_charge_tax_profile_id`, `authority_codes`) ↔ B29, §3.5, §6.9; F-6 ↔ B9, B31, §8.
- **Over-engineering cut:** no second adapter, no locale work, no currency conversion, no tax-exempt flow, no per-line tax override, no rules engine — the data are fixed columns and four small JSON documents; tax lists capped at 4; `apportionMinor` is a thin wrapper over the existing `allocateCappedMinor`.
- **Known gap:** a tax class added after the billing-settings `PUT` can lack a mapping; the hub refuses its bills (`tax_unresolved`) rather than billing them untaxed.

---

## Appendix P — PRA adapter (Punjab, jurisdiction `PK-PB`) ⚠️ Changed in v0.7 (J-6: moved out of the core; content as v0.6.2 unless marked)

Everything in this appendix is specific to the Punjab Revenue Authority's eIMS. The core (§3.15) knows none of it.

### P.1 Placement and capabilities

`apps/hub/src/billing/fiscal/pra.ts`, registered as adapter key **`PRA`**; used by jurisdiction **`PK-PB`** (row A-1 seed). Capabilities: `ordering: 'strict_per_pos_id'` (A12 default), `credit: true` (`InvoiceType` 3). Live in the pilot from day 1 (G1-16, R37); the branch starts on jurisdiction **`PK`** (⚠️ Changed in v0.8.2 (H16-9)) if a go-live precondition is missing and switches by setting.

### P.2 Request mapping (from the neutral submission, §3.15)

- **Transport:** `POST` JSON to the configured environment's base URL (`sandbox` / `production` = `config.environment`; ⚠️ Changed in v0.7.2 (H9-4): the URL arrives as `config.base_url` (branch fiscal settings `base_urls`), else these defaults; sandbox `https://ims.pral.com.pk/ims/sandbox/api/Live/PostData`, production `https://ims.pral.com.pk/ims/production/api/Live/PostData`), header `Authorization: Bearer <credentials.token>` (`credentialNames: ['token']`, ⚠️ v0.7.3 H10-5); Node 22 built-in `fetch` with `AbortSignal.timeout(10_000)`.
- **Identity:** `USIN` = `idempotency_key` (= `invoice_ref`, e.g. `AC1-S000123`, `AC1-C000007`; unique across both series, H6-1); POS ID = the submission's `pos_id` (a credit: its original's, H6-8); the seller's NTN = `seller.tax_registrations` code `NTN` (H9-4, H9-5); PRA needs no `adapter_settings` (its `settingsSchema` accepts `{}` only); date-time from `issued_at`.
- **Invoice type:** `InvoiceType` 1 for `submitSale`; **3 for `submitCredit`, with `RefUSIN` = `original.invoice_ref`**.
- **Totals:** `TotalSaleValue` (Σ item sale values excl. tax and discount), `TotalTaxCharged` (= `invoice.tax_minor`), `Discount`, `TotalBillAmount` (incl. rounding: **the rounding adjustment is inside `TotalBillAmount` only** — never an item, never in `TotalSaleValue` or `TotalTaxCharged` — until A13 / A14 say otherwise, ⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25)).
- **Items:** one per invoice line (description, quantity, unit price, sale value, tax rate, tax); every `PK-PB` profile has one tax, so each line's single `taxes[0]` gives the item's rate and tax (Σ item tax = `TotalTaxCharged`, H6-4); a profile with more than one tax is not seeded for `PK-PB`, and the adapter refuses such a submission as `rejected` (`multi_tax_unsupported`) rather than guess. The **service charge** is one item `Service charge` with its own tax.
- **`PaymentMode`:** each payment's `authority_code` (the mode's `PRA` entry in `authority_codes`, row A-1; seeded `cash` → 1, `card` → 2): 1 cash, 2 card, 3 gift voucher, 4 loyalty card, 5 mixed, 6 cheque; payments of more than one code → **5 (mixed)**, applied only when every payment has a code; a missing code → `rejected` (`mode_unmapped`) (H7-4, H8-3). Wallet (JazzCash, EasyPaisa), QR (Raast) and online transfer have **no code** until A13: such modes are refused on a `PK-PB` branch (`PAYMENT_MODE_NOT_FISCAL`).
- **Buyer:** above the threshold, buyer name and the `tax_id` sent in the CNIC or NTN field by its `type`; a credit sends its original's buyer (read from the original's `invoice_buyers` row, H7-10); a waived bill sends the fields empty (A7 default).
- **Verification QR:** `https://reg.pra.punjab.gov.pk/IMSFiscalReport/SearchPOSInvoice_Report.aspx?PRAInvNo=<fiscal_number>` (the `PK-PB` `receipt_fiscal.qr_url_template`) unless PRA's answer carries its own payload.
- Every other field and the answer's exact shape are taken from PRA's "Technical Specification Software Fiscal Device v1.2" at build; a field the document leaves unclear is a **STOP to the owner**, not a guess.

### P.3 Answer mapping

2xx with a PRA invoice number → `ok { fiscal_number, qr_payload }`; network error, timeout, 5xx → `retry` (`timeout`, `http_503`, …); 4xx or a validation answer → `rejected` (short code, HTTP status); a re-sent USIN PRA already holds → `duplicate` with the number when PRA returns it, else `rejected` (A10 default).

### P.4 Pakistan seed (row A-1)

`PK-PB`: `PKR` (exponent 2); prices exclusive; rounding 100, `nearest`, `all`; `higher_rate` (A6); buyer tax ID above **Rs 50,000 = 5 000 000 paisa** pre-tax after discount (eIMS Rules 2019 r.6), types **CNIC** (13 digits, dashes stripped) and **NTN** (7–9 alphanumerics), printed in full on that customer's receipt (owner Q-1); receipt fields "PRA invoice no.", "NTN", QR; catch-up window **7 days** (r.8(3); the fault is reported at once, r.8(1), by the managers who get the stuck alert); credit support `credit_invoice` (A4: no credit time limit found); profile `restaurant_service` → one tax `PST` (Punjab sales tax on services): digital (card / wallet / QR) **500 bp**, cash **1600 bp**, `PRA.32-24/2026/1313`, 2026-02-19. Rows marked "ref not found" in the research are not seeded. Other provinces' authorities (SRB, KPRA, BRA, FBR-ICT) are milestone C (R37), each with its own adapter and seed.

### P.5 STOP at build

The PRA adapter task (§11 task 30) does not start until **A13** and PRA's spec v1.2 settle the missing `PaymentMode` codes and the rounding / service-charge placement (H6-4).

### P.6 Authority questions (moved from §12.3)

Open; defaults are the strictest / safest; answers change data or this adapter, never the core flow. A1–A7 as G1 §5 (A1 one POS ID per hub, per-device mapping ready; A2 local number + "fiscal number pending" + date / time; A3 QR at the foot, largest size, no logo; A4 no credit time limit; A5 platform orders not fiscalized; A6 `higher_rate`; A7 waiver with manager approval, `buyer_id_refused = true`, buyer fields empty). **New:**

| # | Question | Default until answered |
|---|---|---|
| A8 | How must an amount / equal split be reported — one invoice per payer with proportional lines, or one invoice with several payments? | one invoice per split with proportional lines (OQ-5 (a)) |
| A9 | Is the service charge part of the taxable value, and under which rate? | taxable under `service_charge_tax_profile_id` (owner sets it; the safe setting is the restaurant class) |
| A14 ⚠️ Changed in v0.8 (§25) | How PRA wants the untaxed rounding adjustment: its own field, or only inside `TotalBillAmount`? (it is never part of taxable value) | inside `TotalBillAmount` only (P.2) |
| A13 ⚠️ Changed in v0.6 (H6-4) | PRA `PaymentMode` codes for wallet (JazzCash, EasyPaisa), QR (Raast) and online transfer; whether rounding and service charge are items or bill-level fields | **none — STOP the PRA adapter task (§11 task 30) until answered**; meanwhile such modes are refused on a PRA branch (`PAYMENT_MODE_NOT_FISCAL`, ⚠️ Changed in v0.6.1 (H7-4)) |
| A12 ⚠️ Changed in v0.3 (H1-17) | Must invoices reach PRA in invoice-number order per POS ID? | strict order (the head row blocks) |
| A10 | What does PRA answer to a re-sent USIN it already accepted (crash between send and ack)? | treat an answer carrying the fiscal number as success; anything else → `rejected` + alert, resolved by hand |

A11 was never assigned.

### P.7 Adapter tests

- **P-T1** (was #74) PRA adapter fixtures (recorded from the research's shapes and PRA spec v1.2 at build): sale (cash, card, mixed → `PaymentMode` 1 / 2 / 5), credit (`InvoiceType` 3 + `RefUSIN`), buyer fields above the threshold, 5xx → retry, 4xx → rejected, timeout → retry, duplicate USIN → per A10 default; the Bearer header comes from `fiscal_credentials` (v0.2, OQ-3); no network in the gate.
- **P-T2** (was the PRA part of #74b) `PaymentMode` from the `PRA` code (cash 1, card 2, two codes → 5); Σ item tax = `TotalTaxCharged`; the service-charge item; a credit carries its original's buyer and POS ID, `InvoiceType` 3 and `RefUSIN`; a multi-tax submission → `rejected` (`multi_tax_unsupported`).
- Tests #69–#78b (core) run against both the fake adapter and this one; no network in the gate (recorded fixtures); the live sandbox run is the owner's manual step (§11 #46, G1-12).
